R 65,T 5

((naṉṉūl mūlamum, mayilainātaruraiyum))

((naṉṉūṟ ciṟappuppāyiram))

malartalai yulakiṉ malkiru ḷakala
ilakoḷi parappi yāvaiyum viḷakkum
parutiyi ṉorutā ṉāki mutalī
ṟoppaḷa vācai muṉivikan tuyarnta
aṟputa mūrttitaṉ ṉalartaru taṉmaiyiṉ {{5}}
maṉaviru ḷiriya māṇporuṇ muḻuvatum
muṉivaṟa varuḷiya mūvaṟu moḻiyuḷum
kuṇakaṭal kumari kuṭakam vēṅkaṭam
eṉunāṉ kellaiyi ṉiruntamiḻk kaṭaluḷ
arumporu ḷaintaiyum yāvaru muṇarat {{10}}
tokaivakai viriyiṟ ṟarukeṉat tuṉṉār
ikalaṟa nūṟi yirunila muḻuvatum
taṉateṉak kōlit taṉmata vāraṇam
ticaitoṟu niṟuviya tiṟaluṟu tolcīrk
karuṅkaḻal veṇkuṭaik kārnikar vaṇkait {{15}}
tiruntiya ceṅkōṟ cīya kaṅkaṉ
aruṅkalai vinōta ṉamarā paraṇaṉ
moḻintaṉa ṉāka muṉṉōr nūliṉ
vaḻiyē naṉṉūṟ peyariṉ vakuttaṉaṉ
poṉmatiṟ caṉakaic caṉmati muṉiyaruḷ {{20}}
paṉṉaruñ ciṟappiṟ pavaṇanti
eṉṉu nāmat tiruntavat tōṉē. eṉpatu ciṟappuppāyiram. itaṟkup poruḷ mēlē collutum.

{{(piratipētam)}} pariti. muḻutum

((pāyiram))

((pāyirattiṉ peyarkaḷ))

1

mukavurai patika maṇinturai nūṉmukam
puṟavurai tanturai puṉainturai pāyiram.

((WFW)): {{(itaṉ poruḷ.)}} ivai pāyirattiṟkuk kāraṇap peyarkaḷām eṉṟavāṟu.

{{pi-m}} pāyirattiṟkuk kāraṇap peyarām.

((pāyirattiṉ vakai))

2

pāyiram potucciṟap peṉaviru pāṟṟē.

((WFW)): mēṟcoṉṉapāyiram potuppāyiramum ciṟappuppāyiramumeṉa iruvakaippaṭum e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): avaṟṟuḷ, potuppāyiramāvatu ellā nūṉmukattum uraikkappaṭuvateṉavum, ciṟappuppāyiramāvatu oru nūṉmukattu uraikkappaṭuvateṉavum koḷka.

((potuppāyiram))

3

nūlē nuvalvō ṉuvalun tiṟaṉē
koḷvōṉ kōṭaṟ kūṟṟā maintum
ellā nūṟku miyaippotup pāyiram.

((WFW)): ivvaintum ellānūṉmukattum poruntum potuppāyiramām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} mivai

((nūliṉ iyalpu))

4

nūli ṉiyalpē nuvali ṉōriru
pāyiran tōṟṟi mummaiyi ṉoṉṟāy
nāṟporuṭ payattō ṭeḻumatan taḻuvi
5 ṭeṇṇāṉ kuttiyi ṉōttup paṭalam
eṉṉu muṟuppiṉiṟ cūttiraṅ kāṇṭikai
virutti yākum vikaṟpanaṭai peṟumē.

((WFW)): ivviyalpiṉatu nūlāvatu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): ōrirupāyirantōṟṟalāvatu potuppāyiramum ciṟappuppāyiramum muṉṉuṭaittātal. ēṉaippakutikaḷait tattam ilakkaṇaccūttirattuṭkāṇka.

{{pi-m}} nūleṉṟavāṟu tattam cūttirattuṭkāṇka.

((nūliṉ vakai))

5

mutalvaḻi puṭaiyeṉa nūṉmūṉ ṟākum.

((WFW)): immūṉṟukūṟṟatām nūl. e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} cārpeṉa

((mutalnūl))

6

avaṟṟuḷ,-
viṉaiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ
muṉaivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum.

((WFW)): ivvakaiyālvaruvatu mutaṉūlāvatu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} mutaṉūleṉṟavāṟu

((vaḻinūl))

7

muṉṉōr nūliṉ muṭiporuṅ kottup
piṉṉōṉ vēṇṭum vikaṟpaṅ kūṟi
aḻiyā marapiṉatu vaḻinū lākum.

((WFW)): ivvakaiyālvaruvatu vaḻinūlāvatu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} vaḻinūl

((puṭainūl))

8

iruvar nūṟku moruciṟai toṭaṅkit
tiripuvē ṟuṭaiyatu puṭainū lākum.

((WFW)): ippeṟṟiyāṉvaruvatu cārpunūlāvatu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): immūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟāyvaruvatu nūleṉṟaṟika. tollāciriyar cūttiraṅkaḷai īṇṭuvaittup payantatu eṉṉaiyō veṉiṉ, uṇṭu payantatu; yātu? "muṉṉōr moḻiporuḷē yaṉṟi yavarmoḻiyum poṉṉēpōṟ pōṟṟuva meṉpataṟkum - muṉṉōriṉ vēṟunūl ceytumeṉu mēṟkōḷil leṉpataṟkum kūṟupaḻañ cūttirattiṉ kōḷ" eṉpataṉāṟkoḷka.

((nāṟporuṭpayaṉ))

9

aṟamporu ḷiṉpamvī ṭaṭaitaṉūṟ payaṉē.

((WFW)): innāṟporuḷaiyumpeṟutal nūṟkuppayaṉāvatu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

((eḻumatam))

10

eḻuvakai matamē yuṭampaṭaṉ maṟuttal
piṟartam matamēṟ koṇṭu kaḷaivē
tāa ṉāṭṭit taṉātu niṟuppē
iruvar māṟukō ḷorutalai tuṇivē
5 piṟarnūṟ kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭa lēṉaip
piṟitoṭu paṭāaṉ ṟaṉmataṅ koḷalē.

((WFW)): ivveḻumatamumpaṭavarutal nūṟku iyalpu e-ṟu

{{pi-m}} yuṭaṉpaṭal

((pattukkuṟṟam))

11

kuṉṟak kūṟaṉ mikaippaṭak kūṟal
kūṟiyatu kūṟaṉ māṟukoḷak kūṟal
vaḻūuccoṟ puṇarttaṉ mayaṅka vaittal
veṟṟeṉat toṭuttaṉ maṟṟoṉṟu virittal
5. ceṉṟutēyn tiṟuta ṉiṉṟupaya ṉiṉmai
eṉṟivai yīraiṅ kuṟṟa nūṟkē.

((WFW)): ippattukkuṟṟamum cārāmalvaruvatu nūlāvatu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

((pattu aḻaku))

12

curuṅkac collal viḷaṅka vaittal
naviṉṟōrk kiṉimai naṉmoḻi puṇarttal
ōcai yuṭaimai yāḻamuṭait tātal
muṟaiyiṉ vaippē yulakamalai yāmai
5. viḻumiyatu payattal viḷaṅkutā raṇatta
tākuta nūliṟ kaḻakeṉum pattē.

((WFW)): ippattu māṭciyum vēṇṭum nūṟku e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} māṭcimaiyuṭaittātalvēṇṭumnūl

((muppattiraṇṭu utti))

13

nutalip pukuta lōttumuṟai vaippē
tokuttuc cuṭṭal vakuttuk kāṭṭal
muṭittuk kāṭṭaṉ muṭiviṭaṅ kūṟal
tāṉeṭuttu moḻital piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal
5. coṟporuḷ viritta ṟoṭarccoṟ puṇarttal
iraṭṭuṟa moḻita lētuviṉ muṭittal
oppiṉ muṭittaṉ māṭṭeṟin toḻukal
iṟantatu vilakka letiratu pōṟṟal
muṉmoḻintu kōṭal piṉṉatu niṟuttal
10. vikaṟpattiṉ muṭittaṉ muṭintavai muṭittal
uraittu meṉṟa luraittā meṉṟal
orutalai tuṇita leṭuttuk kāṭṭal
eṭutta moḻiyi ṉeyta vaittal
iṉṉa talla tituveṉa moḻital
15. eñciya colli ṉeytak kūṟal
piṟanūṉ muṭintatu tāṉuṭam paṭutal
taṉkuṟi vaḻakka mikaveṭut turaittal
colliṉ muṭivi ṉapporuṇ muṭittal
oṉṟiṉa muṭitta ṟaṉṉiṉa muṭittal
20. uyttuṇara vaippeṉa vuttiyeṇ ṇāṉkē.

((WFW)): ivaimuppattiraṇṭum tantiravuttiyāvaṉa e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): ivai orucārārvēṇṭuvaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟai māṟṟic colluvārum muppattiraṇṭiṉmēlum pala veṉpārumuḷar; avaiyum aṟintukoḷka.

{{pi-m}} māṭṭeṟintoḻital muṭintatu luraippāmeṉṟal. `tāṉuṭaṉpaṭutal oṉṟaṉamuṭittal

((utti iṉṉateṉpatu))

14

nūṟporuḷ vaḻakkoṭu vāyppak kāṭṭi
ēṟpuḻi yaṟintitaṟ kivvakai yāmeṉat
takumvakai celuttuta ṟantira vutti.

((WFW)): ivvāṟuvaḻaṅkutal tantiravuttiyām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} vuttiyāvatu.

((ottilakkaṇam))

15

nēriṉa maṇiyai niralpaṭa vaittāṅ
kōriṉap poruḷai yoruvaḻi vaippa
tōtteṉa moḻipa vuyarmoḻip pulavar.

((WFW)): ivvakaittāvatu ottu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

((paṭalavilakkaṇam))

16

oruneṟi yiṉṟi viraviya poruḷāṟ
potumoḻi toṭariṉatu paṭala mākum.

((WFW)): ippaṭinaṭappatu paṭalamām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} yaṉṟi paṭalamāvatu

((cūttira ilakkaṇam))

17

cilvakai yeḻuttiṟ palvakaip poruḷaic
celva ṉāṭiyiṟ ceṟittiṉitu viḷakkit
tiṭpa nuṭpañ ciṟantaṉa cūttiram.

((WFW)): ippeṟṟiyāṉ varuvaṉa cūttiraṅkaḷ e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} palpayapporuḷaic cūttiraṅkaḷāvaṉa

((cūttiranilai))

18

āṟṟoḻuk,-
karimā nōkkan tavaḷaip pāyttup
paruntiṉ viḻukkā ṭaṉṉacūt tiranilai.

((WFW)): innāṉkuvakaiyāl niṟpaṉavām cūttiraṅkaḷ e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): āṟṟoḻukku eṉpatu, āṟṟunīr toṭarvaṟātu oḻukumatu pōlac cūttiraṅkaḷum tammuḷ iyaipupaṭṭoḻukuvatu. arimānōkkameṉpatu ciṅkanōkkam. ciṅkam nōkkumiṭattu muṉṉaiyāraiyum piṉṉaiyāraiyum nōkkumatupōla iṟantacūttirattiṉōṭum etirnta cūttirattiṉōṭum iyaipupaṭakkiṭappatu. tavaḷaippāytteṉpatu, tavaḷai pāykiṉṟaviṭattu iṭaiyiṭai nilamkiṭappappāyvatupōlac cūttiram iṭaiyiṭṭuppōy iyaipukoḷḷuvatu. paruntiṉviḻukkāṭeṉpatu paruntu naṭuvēviḻuntu tāṉ karutumporuḷai eṭuttukkoṇṭupōvatu pōla ituvum muṭikkappaṭumporuḷai muṭittuppōm iyaipiṉatu.

{{pi-m}} karimāṉ paruntiṉvīḻvaṉṉa.

((cūttiravakai))

19

piṇṭan tokaivakai kuṟiyē ceykai
koṇṭiyal puṟaṉaṭaik kūṟṟaṉa cūttiram.

((WFW)): ikkūṟṟaṉavām cūttiraṅkaḷ e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): ivaṟṟuḷḷē potumutalāṉavikaṟpamellām aṭaṅkumeṉak koḷka.

((uraiyiṉ potu ilakkaṇam))

20

pāṭaṅ karuttē colvakai coṟporuḷ
tokutturai yutāraṇam viṉāviṭai vicēṭam
virivati kāran tuṇivu payaṉō
ṭāciriya vacaṉameṉ ṟīrē ḻuraiyē.

((WFW)): pāṭamcollalum, karutturaittalum, colvakuttalum coṟporuḷ uraittalum, poḻippuraittalum, utāraṇaṅkāṭṭalum, viṉāttōṟṟalum, viṭaikoṭuttalum, vicēṭaṅkāṭṭalum, virivukāṭṭalum, atikāravaravukāṭṭalum, tuṇivukūṟalum, payaṉoṭupaṭuttalum, āciriyavacaṉaṅkāṭṭalumeṉṉum ippatiṉāṉkupakutiyāṉum uraikkappaṭum, cūttirapporuḷ e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): tokuttuk kaṇṇaḻittal virittuk koṇarnturaittaleṉṉum irukūṟum, poḻippu, akalam, nuṭpameṉṉu mūvakaiyum, eṭuttukkōṭal, pataṅkāṭṭal, patamvirittal, patapporuḷuraittal, viṉātal, viṭuttaleṉṉum aṟukūṟum, poḻippu, akalam, nuṭpam, nūleccam, patapporuḷuraittal, ēṟpuḻikkāṭṭal, eṇṇaleṉṉu mēḻum. "collē colvakai coṟporuḷ cōtaṉai, `maṟainilai yilēcecca nōkkē tuṇivē, karuttē celuttaleṉ ṟīraintu kiḷaviyu, neṟippaṭa varuvatu paṉuvaluraiyē" eṉṉum ippattum, cūttirantōṟṟal, colvakuttal, coṟporuḷuraittal, viṉātal, viṭuttal, vicēṭaṅkāṭṭal, utāraṇaṅkāṭṭal, āciriyavacaṉaṅkāṭṭal, atikāravaravukāṭṭal, tokuttumuṭittal, virittukkāṭṭal, tuṇivukūṟal, payaṉoṭu puṇarttaleṉṉum ippatiṉmuppakutiyumāṉa immata vikaṟpamellām ippatiṉāṉkiṉuḷḷē aṭaṅkumeṉak koḷka.

{{pi-m}} colvakutturaittalum coṟporuḷuṇarttalum; coṟporuḷvirittalum, virittuttērnturaittal maṟainilaiyecca nōkkē cūttiram vakuttal aiyantōṟṟal atikāravaṭaivu

((kāṇṭikaiyurai))

21

karuttup patapporuḷ kāṭṭu mūṉṟiṉum
avaṟṟoṭu viṉāviṭai yākka lāṉum
cūttirat tuṭporu ṭōṟṟuva kāṇṭikai.

((WFW)): ivviruvakaiyāṉum varuvaṉa kāṇṭikaikaḷ e-ṟu. kāṭṭāvatu utāraṇam.

{{pi-m}} ivvakaiyāṉ varuvaṉa.

((viruttiyurai))

22

cūttirat tuṭporu ḷaṉṟiyu māṇṭaik
kiṉṟi yamaiyā yāvaiyum viḷaṅkat
taṉṉurai yāṉum piṟanū lāṉum
aiya makalavaiṅ kāṇṭikai yuṟuppoṭu
5. meyyiṉai yeñcā ticaippatu virutti.

((WFW)): ivviti yaṉaittumuḷḷatu viruttiyāvatu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} yamaiyātiyāvaiyum yaṉaittumvirutti

((nūleṉṉum peyarkkāraṇam))

23

pañcitaṉ collāp paṉuva viḻaiyākac
ceñcoṟ pulavaṉē cēyiḻaiyā - eñcāta
kaiyēvā yākak katirē matiyāka
maiyilā nūṉmuṭiyu māṟu.

24

urattiṉ vaḷamperukki yuḷḷiya tīmaip
purattiṉ vaḷamurukkik pollā - marattiṉ
kaṉakkōṭṭan tīrkkunūlaḵtēpōṉ māntar
maṉakkōṭṭan tīrkkunūṉ māṇpu.

(ivaṟṟiṉporuḷ.) ivvāṟu nūṟkappaṭutalāṉum, nūlēpōlac ceppañceytalāṉum nūleṉappaṭum e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): nūleṉṟa coṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟiṉaveṉak koḷka.

{{pi-m}} mākkaḷ

((2. āciriyariyalpu))

((nallāciriyar))

25

kulaṉaru ṭeyvaṅ koḷkai mēṉmai
kalaipayi ṟeḷivu kaṭṭurai vaṉmai
nilamalai niṟaikōṉ malarnikar māṭciyum
ulakiya laṟivō ṭuyarkuṇa miṉaiyavum
5. amaipava ṉūlurai yāciri yaṉṉē.

26

terivarum perumaiyun tiṇmaiyum poṟaiyum
paruva muyaṟci yaḷaviṟ payattalum
maruviya naṉṉila māṇpā kummē.

27

aḷakka lākā vaḷavum poruḷum
tuḷakka lākā nilaiyu mikuniṟaik kōṟkē.
vaṟappiṉum vaḷantarum vaṇmaiyu malaikkē.

28

aiyan tīrap poruḷai yuṇarttalum
meynnaṭu nilaiyu mikuniṟaik kōṟkē.

29

maṅkala māki yiṉṟi yamaiyā
tiyāvaru makiḻntu mēṟkoḷa melkip
poḻutiṉ mukamalar vuṭaiyatu pūvē.

((WFW)): ivviyalpiṉaiyuṭaiyār kaṟkappaṭum āciriyar e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} kaṟpikkappaṭumāciriyar.

((āciriyarallātavar))

30

moḻikuṇa miṉmaiyu miḻikuṇa viyalpum
aḻukkā ṟavāvañca macca māṭalum
kaḻaṟkuṭa maṭaṟpaṉai paruttik kuṇṭikai
muṭatteṅ koppeṉa muraṇkoḷ cintaiyum
5 uṭaiyō rilarā ciriyarā kutalē.

31

peytamuṟai yaṉṟip piṟaḻa vuṭaṉṟarum
ceyti kaḻaṟpey kuṭattiṉ cīrē.

32

tāṉē tarakkoḷi ṉaṉṟit taṉpāl
mēvik koḷakkoṭā viṭattatu maṭaṟpaṉai.

33

aritiṟ peyakkoṇ ṭapporu ṭāṉpiṟark
keḷitī villatu paruttik kuṇṭikai.

34

palvakai yutavi vaḻipaṭu paṇpiṉ
nallōroḻit,
tallōrk kaḷikku matumuṭat teṅkē.

((WFW)): ippeṟṟik kuṟṟaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyōr kaṟkappaṭātōr e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} koppaṉa peytal paṇpiṉallōrkku

((3. pāṭañcolvataṉ ilakkaṇam))

35

īta liyalpē yiyampuṅ kālaik
kālamu miṭaṉum vāliti ṉōkkic
ciṟantuḻi yiruntutaṉ ṟeyvam vāḻtti
uraikkap paṭuporuḷ ḷuḷḷat tamaittu
5. viraiyāṉ vekuḷāṉ virumpi mukamalarntu
koḷvōṉ koḷvakai yaṟintava ṉuḷaṅkoḷak
kōṭṭamiṉ maṉattiṉūl koṭutta leṉpa.

((WFW)): ivvāṟaḷittal ītaliyalpu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} paṭumporuḷ iyalpu

((4. māṇākkar))

36

taṉmaka ṉācāṉ makaṉē maṉmakaṉ
poruṇaṉi koṭuppōṉ vaḻipaṭu vōṉē
uraikō ḷāḷaṟ kuraippatu nūlē.

((WFW)): kaṟpikkappaṭumāṇakkarāvārivar e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): avaṟṟuḷ, mutaṉūlāṟpeyarpeṟṟaṉa āriyapaṭalam, pārata mutalāyiṉa. karuttaṉāṟ peyarpeṟṟaṉa akattiyam, tolkāppiya mutalāyiṉa. aḷaviṉāṟ peyarpeṟṟaṉa paṉṉirupaṭalam, nālaṭi nāṉūṟu mutalāyiṉa. mikutiyāṟpeyarpeṟṟaṉa kaḷaviyal mutalāyiṉa. poruḷāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa. akanāṉūṟu, puṟanāṉūṟu mutalāyiṉa. ceyvittōṉāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa cātavākaṉam, iḷantiraiya mutalāyiṉa. taṉmaiyāṟ peyarpeṟṟaṉa cintāmaṇi, cūḷāmaṇi, naṉṉūl mutalāyiṉa. iṭukuṟiyāṟpeyar peṟṟaṉa nikaṇṭu nūl, kaḷaikkōṭṭuttaṇṭu mutalāyiṉa.

54

māṭakkuc cittiramu mānakarkkuk kōpuramum
āṭamaittō ṇallārk kaṇiyumpōl-nāṭimuṉ
aituraiyā niṉṟa vaṇinturaiyai yennūṟkum
peyturaiyā vaittār peritu. e-ṉ. iḵtu acciṟappuppāyirattaic ciṟappittateṉak koḷka. aḵtēl nūlkēṭkappukuntārkkup puṟavuraikēṭṭup payaṉ eṉṉai yōveṉiṉ, koḻucceṉṟavaḻit tuṉṉūci iṉitu cellumāṟupōlap parupporuṭṭākiya pāyiraṅkēṭṭārkku nuṇporuṭṭākiya nūl iṉitu viḷaṅkumeṉpataṉālum, `cilaveḻuttiṉāṉiyaṉṟa nūṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta pāyiramaṟiyātāy! palaveḻuttiṉāṉākiya nūl yāṅṅaṉam aṟitiyō? pētāy' eṉṟu ikaḻappaṭutalāṉum ivviruvakaippāyiramum vēṇṭum eṉka. ippāyiraccūttiraṅkaḷai viritteḻutiṟ pākkumeṉpataṉāṉāṉum perumpālum tāmē poruḷviḷaṅkikkiṭattalāṉum urai viritteḻutiṟṟilam ; ivaṟṟai viḷaṅka viritturaittukkoḷka.

{{pi-m}} māṇakarkku vaṇinturaiyē cilleḻuttiṉāṉ palleḻuttiṉāṉ

iṉit tamiḻiṉatutaṉmaiyum innūṟkuc ciṟappuppāyiramum collutum. malartalai yulakiṉ iruntavat tōṉē.

{{pi-m}} tamiḻatu eṉpatu, tamiḻiṉatu taṉmaiyum innūṟkuc ciṟappuppāyiramum uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): malar talai ulakiṉ - paranta iṭattaiyuṭaiya pūmiyiṉkaṇ, malku iruḷ akala - ceṟinta iruḷnīṅka, ilaku oḷi parappi - viḷaṅkā niṉṟakatiraivirittu, yāvaiyum viḷakkum - nilam nīr mutalākiya ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum viḷaṅkakkāṭṭum, - parutiyiṉ - ātittaṉaippōla, oru tāṉāki - ulakiṟkellām tāṉ oruvaṉēyāki, mutal īṟu oppu aḷavu ācai muṉivu ikantu uyarnta - piṟappum iṟappum uvamaiyum aḷavum ārvamum ceṟṟamumiṉṟi ñāṉakuṇaṅkaḷāṉ mikka, aṟputamūrtti - āccariyattaiyuṭaiya iṟaivaṉāṉavaṉ, taṉ alartaru taṉmaiyiṉ - taṉṉuṭaiya paravāniṉṟa āṟṟalāṉē (taṉṉuṭaiya virinta taṉmaiyākiya karuṇaiyeṉiṉumamaiyum), maṉa iruḷ iriya - ulakattuḷḷa uyirkaḷiṉuṭaiya maṉaṅkaḷiluṇṭāṉa aṟiyāmaiyākiya iruḷ nīṅka, māṇporuḷ muḻuvatum - māṭcimaippaṭṭa aṟa mutaṟporuḷ aṉaittiṉaiyum, muṉivu aṟa aruḷiya - (patiṉeṇṇilattiluḷḷār ellārum) virumpa aruḷicceyta, mūvaṟu moḻiyuḷum - patiṉeṭṭu moḻikaḷuḷḷum, kuṇakaṭal kumari kuṭakam vēṅkaṭam eṉum nāṉku ellaiyiṉ iru tamiḻ kaṭaluḷ - ic coṉṉa nāṉku karaikkum uṭpaṭṭu vaḻaṅkāniṉṟa periya tamiḻākiya kaṭaliluḷḷa, aru poruḷ aintaiyum - eḻuttuc coṟporuḷ yāppu aṇiyeṉṉum ariya poruḷaintaiyum, yāvarum uṇara - ellārum uṇarumpaṭi, tokai vakai viriyiṉ taruka eṉa - immummaiyāṉum viḷaṅkaccolluka eṉa, tuṉṉār ikal aṟa nūṟi - poruntātāruṭaiya māṟupāṭellām māyumpaṭi keṭuttu, iru nilam muḻuvatum - peritākiya pūmi aṉaittiṉaiyum, taṉateṉa kōli - taṉateṉṟē paṟṟikkoṇṭu, taṉ mata vāraṇam - taṉakkēyuriya matayāṉaiyai, ticaitoṟum niṟuviya - eṭṭuttikkilum ellaikkuṟiyāka niṟuttiya, tiṟal uṟu tol cīr - valiyōṭuporuntiya ulakuḷḷatāṉa aṉṟu toṭaṅki iṭaiyaṟātu vārāniṉṟa pukaḻiṉaiyum, karu kaḻal - kūṭātāraik koṉṟu kaṭṭiṉa kuraikaḻaliṉaiyum, veṇkuṭai - paṟṟalar pakarum paḻimā caṟuttu maṟṟulakaḷikkum maṇimuttukkuṭaiyiṉaiyum, kārnikar vaṇkai - viḻaipayaṉkarutātu mētiṉiyavarkku maḻaipōlutavum māmalarkkaiyiṉaiyum, tiruntiya ceṅkōl - kuṟṟamaṟṟulakiṟ koṭuṅkaliturantu ceppamvaḷarkkum ceyyakōliṉaiyumuṭaiyavaṉāṉa, cīya kaṅkaṉ - taṅkalarkulamām veṅkayamaṉuṅkum ciṅkamākiya kaṅkaṉeṉpāṉ, aru kalai vinōtaṉ - ariyanūlkaḷai ārāytalē taṉakku iṉpamākavuṭaiyāṉ, amarāparaṇaṉ - porum pōriṉaiyē puṉaikalaṉākavuṭaiyāṉeṉṉum aḻakamai peyariṉaiyuṭaiyāṉ, moḻintaṉaṉāka - ēva, muṉṉōr nūliṉ vaḻiyē - paḻaiyōrcoṉṉa nūlkaḷiṉ paṭiyē, naṉṉūṟpeyariṉ - naṉṉūleṉṉum peyariṉāṉē, vakuttaṉaṉ - ceytaṉaṉ; avaṉ yāvaṉeṉiṉ, - poṉ matil caṉakai - poṉmatil puṭaicūḻnta caṉanātapurattu, caṉmati muṉiyaruḷ - caṉmatiyeṉṉum naṉmuṉiyaḷitta, paṉ aru ciṟappiṉ - collutaṟkariya peruntaṉmaiyaiyum, pavaṇanti eṉṉum nāmattu - pavaṇantiyeṉṉum periya peyariṉaiyum, irutavattōṉ - periya tavattiṉaiyumuṭaiyōṉ e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): `kuṇakaṭal hahahahaha ellaiyiṉ:' iḵtu ivvellaiyuḷ naṭakkumeṉa ellai pōntatu. `iruntamaḻ hahahahaha aintaiyum' eṉa nutaliya poruḷ pōntatu. `yāvaru muṇara': ellārumuṇartal payaṉākaveṉap payaṉ pōntatu. `tokaivakai viri' eṉa yāppup pōntatu. `tarukeṉahahahahaha moḻintaṉaṉāka:' aruṅkalai vinōtaṉākaiyālē tamiḻiṉ arumaiperumaikaḷaiyaṟintu ellārum iṉitaṟiya viḷaṅkacceykaveṉṟu ivaṉ ēvutalālēyeṉak kāraṇam pōntatu. `muṉṉōr nūliṉ vaḻiyē' eṉa vaḻi pōntatu. `naṉṉūṟ peyariṉ vakuttaṉaṉ' eṉa nūṟpeyar pōntatu. `poṉmatil hahahahahatavattōṉē' eṉa ākkiyōṉ peyar pōntatu. `tuṉṉārikalaṟanūṟihahahahahamoḻintaṉaṉāka' eṉavē ivaṉkālatteṉavum ivaṉ avaikkaḷatteṉavum ivaṉ avaiyiluḷḷār kēṭṭāreṉavum kālamum kaḷaṉum kēṭṭāroṭum pōntaṉa. ivvāṟṟāṉ itaṟkup patiṉorupāyiramum pōntavāṟu itaṉakattē kaṇṭukoḷka.

{{pi-m}} mutaliya ulakellām tāṉ oruvaṉumēyāki ārvum; ācaiyum aṟamporuḷiṉpa maṉaittaiyum patiṉeṇṇilat tuḷḷārum ellaikkum tamiḻeṉṉum periya tuṭaṅki muṟṟulaku viḷaipayaṉ ; viṉaippayaṉ veṅkayanuṅkuñ avaṉēva periya peyariṉāṉē caṉanākapurattu ; caṉakāpurattu peruntaṉmaiyaiyuṭaiya tamiḻpporuḷ nutaliṟṟeṉpatu.

((naṉṉūṟ ciṟappuppāyira muṟṟiṟṟu.))

((mutalāvatu eḻuttatikāram))

((1. eḻuttiyal))

((kaṭavuḷvaṇakkamum atikāramum))

55

pūmali yacōkiṉ puṉainiḻa lamarnta
nāṉmukaṟ ṟoḻutunaṉ kiyampuva ṉeḻuttē.

e-tu cūttiram. ivvatikāram eṉṉutali eṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭatō veṉiṉ, atikāra nutaliyatūum atikārattiṉatu peyaruraippavē viḷaṅkum. āyiṉ, ivvatikāram eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, eḻuttilakkaṇa muṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ eḻuttatikārameṉṉum peyarttu. eḻutappaṭutaliṉ eḻutteṉak koḷka. atikārameṉṟatu muṟaimai, e-ṟu. ivvatikārattuḷ eṉaivakai yōttiṉāṉ eḻuttilakkaṇamuṇarttiṉāṉōveṉiṉ, aivakai yōttiṉāṉ uṇarttiṉāṉeṉka. avaṟṟuḷ, immutalōttu eṉṉutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, ōttunutaliyatūum ōttiṉatu peyaruraippavē viḷaṅkum. āyiṉ, ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, eṇ peyar mutalāṉa eḻuttilakkaṇam pattum uṇarttiṟṟākalāṉ, eḻuttiyaleṉṉum peyarttu. ivvōttiṉuḷ, ittalaiccūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, orucārār vēṇṭum ciṟappuppāyiram uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. eṉṉai? "vaṇakka matikāra meṉṟiraṇṭuñ collac, ciṟappeṉṉum pāyira mām" (yā. vi. ka, urai) eṉṟārākaliṉ. `pūmalihahahahaṟoḻutu' eṉa vaṇakkam coṉṉavāṟu. `naṉkiyampuvaṉeḻuttu' eṉa atikāram coṉṉavāṟu. aḵtēl, nūl nuvalvāṉ pukuntu īṇṭu vaṇakkam vaikkavēṇṭiyatu eṉṉaiyōveṉiṉ, naṉṟu coṉṉāy! "vaḻipaṭu teyva vaṇakkañ ceytu, maṅkala moḻi mutalāka vakuttē, yeṭuttuk koṇṭa vilakkaṇa vilakkiyam, iṭukka ṇiṉṟi yiṉitu muṭiyum" (yā. vi. ka, urai) eṉpavākaliṉ, īṇṭu vaṇakkam ceyyappaṭṭatu.

((WFW)): pūmali acōkiṉ, e-tu pūkkaḷāl mikkuḷḷa acōkiṉatu e-ṟu. puṉai niḻal amarnta, e-tu alaṅkariyāniṉṟa niḻalilē eḻuntaruḷiyirunta e-ṟu. nāṉmukaṉtoḻutu, e-tu nāṉku tirumukaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyāṉai vaṇaṅki e-ṟu. naṉku iyampuvaṉ, e-tu aḻakitākac colluvaṉ e-ṟu. eḻuttu, e-tu eḻuttilakkaṇattai e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): ēkāram īṟṟacai. patapporuḷuraittavāṟu. eṉṉai? "eṉṉa colliṉu miyaṉṟa maruṅkiṟ, paṉṉi yuraippatu patapporu ḷākum" eṉṟārākaliṉ.

(poḻippurai.) nālvakaippūkkaḷiṉāl mikkuṭaiya acōkiṉatu taṉ ellaiyai ikavātē eppoḻutum alaṅkariyā niṉṟa niḻalilē eḻuntaruḷiyirunta nāṉmukat toruvaṉai maṉamoḻi meykaḷiṉ makiḻntuṭaṉ vaṇaṅki īraiṅkuṟṟamumakaṟṟi īraintaḻakoṭum puṇarntu aritiṉṟi viḷaṅka eḻuttilakkaṇattai yāṉ colluvaṉ e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): itu poḻippurai ; eṉṉai? "poḻippeṉap paṭuvatu poruntiya poruḷaip, piṇṭa mākak koṇṭuraip patuvē" (yā. kā. vi. urai) eṉṟārākaliṉ.

{{pi-m}} eṉṉutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ ellaiyai

((eḻuttilakkaṇattiṉ pakuti))

56

eṇpeyar muṟaipiṟap puruva māttirai
mutalī ṟiṭainilai pōliyeṉṟā
patam puṇar peṉappaṉ ṉirupāṟ ṟatuvē.

e-ṉ, mēl eḻuttilakkaṇamiyampuvaṉeṉṟār; aḵtu ittuṇaip pakutiyāṉ uṇarttappaṭumeṉṟu atupaṭum pākupāṭuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): eḻuttukkaḷatu tokaiyum peyarum muṟaiyum piṟappum vaṭivum aḷavum moḻikku mutalāvaṉavum īṟāvaṉavum iṭaiyāvaṉavum pōliyumeṉṉum ivveḻuttuk kūṟupāṭu pattum ivveḻuttāṉām patamum appatam tammōṭum urupōṭum puṇarum puṇarcciyumeṉap paṉṉiru pakutiyiṉaiyuṭaittu, mēṟcoṉṉa eḻuttilakkaṇam e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): eṉṟāveṉpatu eṇṇiṭaiccol. ēkāram īṟṟacai.

{{pi-m}} puṇarppeṉa poruṭpākupāṭu pakutittu

((1. eḻuttiṉ eṇ))

57

moḻimutaṟ kāraṇa māmaṇut tiraḷoli
eḻuttatu mutalcār peṉaviru vakaittē.

e-ṉ, iyampappeṟum eḻuttāvatu iṉṉateṉpatūum ataṉ pākupāṭum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): moḻikku mutaṟkāraṇamākiya paramāṇukkaḷatu iyaipiṉāṉ ākuvatāṉa oli eḻutteṉappaṭum; atu mutaleḻuttum cārpeḻuttumeṉa iraṇṭukūṟām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): īṇṭu oliyeḻuttuc ciṟappuṭaimaiyiṉ eṭuttōtiṉār. eṉṉai? "ciṟappuṭaip poruḷait tāṉeṭuttu moḻital" eṉpatu tantiravuttiyākalāṉ. orucārārvēṇṭum uṇarveḻuttu mutalāṉa vikaṟpamellām ivvatikārap puṟanaṭai (cū. 256, urai)yuṭ kaṇṭu koḷka.

{{pi-m}} mutaleḻuttuc cārpeḻutteṉa

((mutaleḻuttu))

58

uyiru muṭampumā muppatu mutalē.

e-ṉ, mutaleḻuttāvaṉa uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): uyireṉṟum uṭampeṉṟum collappaṭṭa muppateḻuttum mutaleṉappaṭum e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): eṉavē mutaleḻutteṉat tokaiyāṉ oṉṟum, uyireḻuttu uṭampeḻutteṉa vakaiyāṉ iraṇṭum, uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum uṭampu patiṉeṭṭumeṉṟu vakuttukkūṭṭa viriyāṉ muppatumām, mutaleḻuttu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} mutaleḻutteṉappaṭum.

((cārpeḻuttu))

59

uyirmey yāyta muyiraḷa poṟṟaḷa
paḵkiya i u ai au maḵkāṉ
taṉinilai pattuñ cārpeḻut tākum.

e-ṉ, cārpeḻuttāvaṉa uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): uyirmeyyum, āytamum, uyiraḷapeṭaiyum, oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyum, kuṟṟiyalikaramum, kuṟṟiyalukaramum, aikārakkuṟukkamum, aukārakkuṟukkamum, makarakkuṟukkamum, āytakkuṟukkamu meṉṉum ippattum cārpeḻuttām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): eṉṉai? "eḻutteṉap paṭupa, akaramuta ṉakara viṟuvāy muppaḵteṉpa cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē" (tol. nūṉ. 1) eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum ivvāṟu mutalcārpeṉa vakuttuk koṇṭāreṉka. aḵtē, avar cārpeḻutteṉa mūṉṟē koṇṭārālōveṉiṉ, aḵtē, naṉṟu coṉṉāy! oḻintavai eppāṟpaṭumeṉṟārkku mūṉṟāvatōr pakuti collalāvatiṉmaiyāṉum mutaleḻuttāntaṉmai avaṟṟiṟkiṉmaiyāṉum cārpiṟṟōṉṟutalāṉum ippattum cārpākavē koḷḷavēṇṭumeṉpatu. aḵtē amaika. oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyum aikārakkuṟukkamum aukārakkuṟukkamum makarakkuṟukkamum āytak kuṟukkamum yāṇṭup peṟṟāmōveṉiṉ, "vaṉmaiyoṭu raḵkāṉ ḻaḵkā ṉoḻintāṅ, kaṉmey yāytamō ṭaḷapeḻu morōvaḻi," "aḷapeṭai taṉiyiraṇ ṭalvaḻi aiau, uḷatā moṉṟarai taṉimaiyumākum", "patiṉeṇ meyyu matuvē mavvō, ṭāytamu maḷaparai tēytalai murittē" eṉṟār, āciriyar avinayaṉārumeṉak koḷka.

{{pi-m}} ivvāṟu koṇṭāreṉka oḻittāṅku aṉmai aviṉayaṉārum; aviṉaiyiṉārum

((cārpeḻuttiṉ viri))

60

uyirmey yiraṭṭunūṟ ṟeṭṭuya rāytam
eṭṭuyi raḷapeḻu mūṉṟeṟ ṟaḷapeṭai
āṟē ḻaḵku mimmup pāṉēḻ
ukara māṟā ṟaikāṉ mūṉṟē
5 aukā ṉoṉṟē maḵkāṉ mūṉṟē
āyta miraṇṭoṭu cārpeḻut tuṟuviri
oṉṟoḻi munnūṟ ṟeḻupā ṉeṉpa.

e-ṉ, cārpeḻuttiṉ virittokai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): uyirum meyyuṅkūṭṭi uccarikkappaṭāniṉṟa eḻuttu irunūṟṟorupattāṟum, vikārappaṭātuniṉṟa āytameṭṭum, uyiraḷapeṭai irupattoṉṟum, oṟṟaḷapeṭai nāṟpattiraṇṭum, kuṟṟiyalikaram muppattēḻum, kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟum, aikārakkuṟukkam mūṉṟum, aukārakkuṟukkam oṉṟum, makarakkuṟukkam mūṉṟum, āytakkuṟukkam iraṇṭum ākac cārpeḻuttiṉ virittokai munnūṟṟaṟupattoṉpatām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): ikkūṟupāṭukaḷ tattam ilakkaṇac cūttirattuḷḷē kaṇṭukoḷka. eṉavē cārpeḻutteṉat tokaiyāṉ oṉṟum, vakaiyāṟ pattum, viriyāṉ munnūṟṟaṟupattoṉpatumām cārpeḻuttu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): eṉavē iccūttiraṅkaḷāṟ pōnta poruḷ eḻutteṉat tokaiyāṉ oṉṟum, mutaleḻuttuc cārpeḻutteṉa vakaiyāṉ iraṇṭum, ivviraṇṭaṉ viriyumkūṭṭa viriyāṉ munnūṟṟut toṇṇūṟṟoṉpatūumām eḻutteṉpatāyiṟṟu. iṉṉum ivaṟṟaik kuṟṟuyir, neṭṭuyir, uyirmeykkuṟil, uyirmey neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭaiyiṉameṉpavaṟṟāṉum piṟavaṟṟāṉum pakuppa eṉaittum palavām. avaiyum vēṇṭumēl avvāṟu viritturaittukkoḷka. akarameṉat tokaiyāṉoṉṟum, uyir akaramum uyirmey akaramumeṉa vakaiyāṉiraṇṭum, uyir akaramoṉṟum, uyirmey akaram patiṉeṭṭumeṉa viriyāṟ pattoṉpatumām. akaramoḻintaṉavum ivvāṟē koḷka. iṉi, tokai vakaiviriyait tokaivakaiviriyāṉuṟaḻntu oṉpatākki uraippārumuḷar. ivar īṇṭai vaḻakkiṟku vēṇṭumāṟē koṇṭāreṉka. tāmē tamittu niṟkaiyiṉ mutaleḻutteṉṟāyiṉa. avaiyē tammoṭu tām cārntum iṭañcārntum iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum cārntum vikārattālvarutaliṟ cārpeḻutteṉṟāyiṉaveṉakkoḷka. eṇ muṟṟum.

{{pi-m}} cārpeḻuttiṉatu cūttiraṅkaḷuḷḷē mutaleḻuttum cārpeḻuttumeṉa uraippa mutalāyiṉa cārpāyiṉa.

((peyarkaḷiṉ potuvilakkaṇam))

61

iṭukuṟi kāraṇap peyarpotuc ciṟappiṉa.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē peyarāmāṟu uṇarttuvāṉeṭuttuk koṇṭār, avaṟṟuḷ, ittalaiccūttiram peyarkkellām potuvāyatōrilakkaṇamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): iṭukuṟiyāṉum kāraṇattiṉāṉumākum peyarkaḷ, potuvaippaṟṟiyum ciṟappaippaṟṟiyum varuvaṉavām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): iḵtu eṉṉai coṉṉavāṟeṉiṉ, iṭukuṟip potuppeyarum, iṭukuṟicciṟappuppeyarum kāraṇap potuppeyarum, kāraṇac ciṟappuppeyarumeṉa yāvakaippeyarum nālvakaip paṭuvaṉavām e-ṟu. eṉavē, ulakattuppeyarkaḷellām ivvirutiṟattāṉumaṉṟi vārāmai vaḻakkiṭattum ceyyuḷiṭattum yāṇṭum ārāyntukoḷka. āvi, uyir, mey, uṭampu eṉpaṉa iṭukuṟip potuppeyar, avaṟṟuḷ, a, ā; ka, ṅa eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa iṭukuṟicciṟappuppeyar. kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭaiyiṉameṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattaṉa kāraṇap potuppeyar. kuṟṟikaram, kuṟṟukaram, makarakkuṟukkam, āytakkuṟukkameṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattaṉa kāraṇac ciṟappup peyar. ivvāṟē piṟavum ārāyntaṟika.

{{pi-m}} koṇṭār iccūttiram coṉṉavāṟō ceyyuḷiṭattaṉṟiyum vaḻakkiṭattum yāṇṭum kuṟṟukaram, kuṟṟikaram

((2. eḻuttiṉ peyar))

62

ammuta līrā ṟāvi kammutal
meymmū vāṟeṉa viḷampiṉar pulavar.

e-ṉ, orucār potuppeyarāmāṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): akaramutalākaniṉṟa paṉṉiraṇṭiṉaiyum uyireṉṟum, kakaramutalākaniṉṟa patiṉeṭṭiṉaiyum uṭampeṉṟum peyariṭṭuvaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ, muṟkālattuk kaṟṟuvallōr e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): `viḷampiṉar pulavar' eṉṟa vitappiṉāṉēyum, pāyirattiṟ peṟuvatēyaṉṟi nūluḷḷum iḵtu etirnūlallaveṉpatu peṟṟāmeṉak koḷka.

{{pi-m}} mēṟkālattu

((kuṟil))

63

avaṟṟuḷ,
a i u e okkuṟi laintē. ituvumatu.

((WFW)): āviyeṉṟum meyyeṉṟum mēṟkūṟappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟuḷ, a i u e o eṉṉumaintum kuṟṟeḻutteṉṉum kuṟiyavām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): a i u e o eṉa ivaṟṟai uccarittu aṉṉavātal kaṇṭukoḷka. `avaṟṟuḷ' eṉpataṉaip peyaratikāramuṟṟumaḷavum varuvittukkoḷka.

{{pi-m}} eṉṟu collappaṭum ainteḻuttum eṉpataṉai ivvatikāramuṟṟum.

((neṭil))

64

āī ūē aiō auneṭil. ituvumatu.

((VIRI)): ā ī ū ē ō au eṉa uccarittu aṉṉavātal kaṇṭukoḷka.

((cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ))

65

a i ummutaṟ ṟaṉivariṟ cuṭṭē.

e-ṉ, avvuyir/kkē iṉṉum oruvakaiyāṟ peyariṭutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)):a i u eṉṉum immūṉṟum moḻimutaṟkaṇṇē taṉittuniṟpiṟ cuṭṭeṉṉum kuṟiyavām e-ṟu.

{{pi-m}} utāraṇam (piṉṉum ippaṭiyē.)

((viṉāveḻuttukkaḷ))

66

eya mutalum āō vīṟṟum
ēyiru vaḻiyum viṉāvā kummē. ituvumatu.

((WFW)): ekaramum yāvum moḻimutaṟkaṇṇē niṉṟu viṉāvām; ākāramum ōkāramum īṟṟiṉkaṇṇēniṉṟu viṉāvām; ēkāram ivviraṇṭiṭattum niṉṟu viṉāvām. e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): uyirviṉāvuṭaṉē yāviṉāvuraittamai mayaṅkakkūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām piṟaveṉiṉ, itu tokaivakaiviripaṭac ceykiṉṟa nūlātalāṉum mutalviṉāveṉṉum poruḷoppumaiyāṉum kuṟṟamākāteṉak koḷka.

va-ṟu. evaṉ, evaḷ, evar, etu, evai; yāvaṉ, yāvaḷ, yāvar, yātu, yāvai eṉa e yā mutaṟkaṇ viṉāvāy vantaṉa. uṇkā, uṇkō? koṟṟā! (cāttā, tēvā, pūtā) eṉa ākāramum ōkāramum īṟṟiṉkaṇ viṉāvāyvantaṉa. ēvaṉ, ēvaḷ, ēvar, ētu, ēvai; uṇkē? koṟṟā! eṉa ēkāram mutaliṉum īṟṟiṉum niṉṟu viṉāvāyiṟṟu.

{{pi-m}} e-ṉ, ituvumatu mutalviṉāveṉṉum peyaravām īṟṟuviṉāveṉṉum peyaravām iruvaḻiviṉāveṉṉum peyarttām.

((valliṉam))

67

valliṉaṅ kacaṭa tapaṟaveṉa vāṟē.

e-ṉ, mēl meyyeṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ, oru cāraṉavaṟṟiṟku iṉṉum oruvakaiyāṟ peyariṭutal nutaliṟṟu.

{{pi-m}} ōrntuṇarka

((iṉaveḻuttu))

70

aiau iuc ceṟiya mutaleḻut
tivviraṇ ṭōriṉa māy varu muṟaiyē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē muṟaiyāmāṟu uṇarttuvāṉeṭuttuk koṇṭār; avaṟṟuḷ, iccūttiram mutaleḻuttai iṉamaṭaittal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): aikārattai ikarattōṭum, aukārattai ukarattōṭum cērkka, akaramutalāṉa mutaleḻuttellām muṟaiyē ivviraṇṭeḻuttut tammuḷ iṉamām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. aā, iīai, uūau, eē, oō, kaṅa, caña, ṭaṇa, tana, pama, yara, lava, ḻaḷa, ṟaṉa eṉavarum.

{{pi-m}} varaṉmuṟaiyē iī, uū, eē, iai, oō, uau.

((iṉameṉṟataṟkukkāraṇam))

71

tāṉa muyaṟci yaḷavu poruḷvaṭi
vāṉavoṉ ṟātiyōr puṭaiyop piṉamē.

e-ṉ, mēl ivveḻuttu, `ivviraṇṭōriṉamāyvarum' eṉṟār, avai appaṭi varuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): eḻuttukkaḷ tam piṟappiṭamē muyaṟciyē māttiraiyē poruḷē vaṭivē yeṉṟivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟāṉum palavāṉum oru puṭaiyoppaṉavāyiṉ, iṉamām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): aākkaḷ iṭattāṉum muyaṟciyāṉum, aṟāyiram, āṟāyirameṉṉum poruḷāṉum, vaṭivāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa. iīkkaḷ iṭattāṉum muyaṟciyāṉum, irāyiram, īrāyirameṉṉum poruḷāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa; aikāram ivaṟṟuḷ iṭattāṉum muyaṟciyāṉum ottu ikarattōṭu kūṭi iṉamāyiṟṟu. uūkkaḷ iṭattāṉum muyaṟciyāṉum, uṅku, ūṅku eṉṉum poruḷāṉum vaṭivāṉum oru puṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa. o ōkkaḷ, iṭattāṉum muyaṟciyāṉum, ōrāyiram, ōrāyirameṉṉum poruḷāṉum, vaṭivāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa. ka ṅakkaḷ, muyaṟciyāṉum māttiraiyāṉum, kuḷakkarai, kuḷaṅkaraiyeṉṉum poruḷāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa. ca ñakkaḷ, muyaṟciyāṉum māttiraiyāṉum, maccikaṉ, mañcikaṉeṉṉum poruḷāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa. ṭaṇakkaḷ, muyaṟciyāṉum māttiraiyāṉum, maṭkuṭam, maṇkuṭameṉṉum poruḷāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa. ta nakkaḷ muyaṟciyāṉum māttiraiyāṉum, pāḻttūṟu, pāḻntūṟu eṉṉum poruḷāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa. pa makkaḷ, muyaṟciyāṉum māttiraiyāṉum, vēyppuṟam, vēympuṟameṉṉum poruḷāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa. ya rakkaḷ iṭattāṉum māttiraiyāṉum, vēyal, vēraleṉṉum poruḷāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa. la vakkaḷ iṭattāṉum māttiraiyāṉum, ellāvakai, evvakaiyeṉṉum poruḷāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa. ḻa ḷakkaḷ iṭattāṉum māttiraiyāṉum, kāḻakavuṭaiyāṉ, kāḷakavuṭaiyāṉeṉṉum poruḷāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa. ṟa ṉakkaḷ, muyaṟciyāṉum māttiraiyāṉum, naṟku naṉku eṉṉum poruḷāṉum orupuṭaiyottu iṉamāyiṉa.

{{pi-m}} aḷavē orupuṭaiyāṉ

((3. eḻuttiṉmuṟai))

72

ciṟappiṉu miṉattiṉuñ ceṟintīṇ ṭammutal
naṭatta ṟāṉē muṟaiyā kummē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē muṟaiyāmāṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): ciṟappaippaṟṟiyum iṉattaippaṟṟiyum oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟuceṉṟu toṉṟutoṭṭu akaramutalāka ulakattu naṭakkiṉṟa kiṭakkaimuṟaitāṉē nūluḷḷum muṟaiyāvatu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): akaram tāṉēnaṭantum naṭavā uṭampai naṇṇiyum naṭattalāṉum, araṉ ari ayaṉ arukaṉeṉṉum paramar tirunāmattiṟku orumutalāyum, aṟam poruḷiṉpameṉṉum mupporuḷiṉ mutaṟporuṭkum, aruḷ aṉpu aṇi aḻaku mutalāyiṉ naṟporuṭkum mutalāyum varutalāṉum muṉvaikkappaṭṭatu. iṭam muyaṟci poruḷ vaṭivukaḷ orupuṭaiyoppumaiyiṉ akarattiṉpiṉ ākāram vaikkappaṭṭatu. iṭam muyaṟci poruḷkaḷ orupuṭaiyoppumaiyiṉ ikarattiṉpiṉ īkāram vaikkappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟē oḻinta eḻuttukkaḷ tam kiṭakkai muṟaikkāraṇam aṟintukoḷka. muṟai muṟṟum.

{{pi-m}} naṭakkiṉṟamuṟaitāṉē

((4. eḻuttiṉ piṟappu))

73

niṟaiyuyir muyaṟciyi ṉuḷvaḷi turappa
eḻuntaṇut tiraḷuraṅ kaṇṭa mucci
mūkkuṟ ṟitaḻnāp pallaṇat toḻiliṉ
vevvē ṟeḻuttoli yāyvaral piṟappē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappu vēṟupāṭu uṇarttuvāṉeṭuttukkoṇṭār, avaṟṟuḷ, iccūttiram ataṉ potuvitiyuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): vikārappaṭātu taṉṉiyalpiṉiṉṟa uyir, moḻivaleṉṉu muḷḷantōṟṟi muyala uṇṇiṉṟa vaḷi kaṭāva āṇṭu niṉṟa oḷiyaṇuvīṭṭam vicaitteḻuntu neñcē miṭaṟē ucciyē mūkkēyeṉṟinnāṉkiṭattaiyum mutalaṭaintu itaḻē nāvē pallē aṇṇamē eṉṟin nāliṭattaiyum piṉpu aṭaiya ivaṟṟatu muyaṟci vikaṟpattāṟ palvēṟu vakaippaṭṭa eḻuttoliyāyp pulappaṭal eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): eṉṉai? "āṟṟa luṭaiyuyir muyaṟciyi ṉaṇuviyain, tēṟṟaṉa voliyāyt tōṟṟutal piṟappē" eṉṟār āciriyar avinayaṉārumeṉak koḷka.

{{pi-m}} ṟitaṇā piṟavivēṟupāṭu muyaṟciyāṉ.

((mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ iṭappiṟappu))

avvaḻi,

mēvu meṉmaimūk kurampeṟum vaṉmai.

((mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ muyaṟcippiṟappu))

iī eē aiyaṅ kāppō

{{pi-m}} naṉiyuṟiṟ mikavuṟil

((cārpeḻuttiṉ iṭamum muyaṟciyum))

86

āytak kiṭantalai yaṅkā muyaṟci
cārpeḻut tēṉavun tammuta laṉaiya:

e-ṉ, cārpeḻuttiṟku iṭamum muyaṟciyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): āytaveḻuttiṟku iṭam talaiyām; aṅkāntu collutal muyaṟciyām. allāta cārpeḻuttukkaḷukkum iṭa muyaṟcikaḷ tattam mutaleḻuttukkaḷōṭoppaṉavām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. ka kā, eḵku, āa, maṅkalam, kēṇmiyā, nāku, maippuṟam, mauval, varumvaṇikaṉ, kaḵṟītu eṉa muṟaiyē uccarittuk kaṇṭukoḷka.

{{pi-m}} cārpeḻuttukkaḷukku iṭaṅkāraṇaṅkaḷ; cārpeḻuttukkaḷ iṭamuyaṟcik kāraṇaṅkaḷil

((piṟappiṉpuṟanaṭai))

87

eṭuttal paṭutta ṉalita luḻappil
tiripun tattamiṟ ciṟituḷa vākum.

e-ṉ, mēluraittavaṟṟiṟku oru puṟanaṭai yuṇarttutalnutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): palaveḻuttukkaḷukkum tāṉamuyaṟcikaḷ oṉṟākac collappaṭṭaṉavēṉum, eṭuttal paṭuttal nalital mutalāṉa uḻappuvikaṟpaṅkaḷiṉāṉē oṉṟukkoṉṟu ciṟitu vēṟupāṭuṭaiyavām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): utāraṇam, muṉṉarkkāṭṭiṉavaṟṟuṭ kaṇṭukoḷka.

{{pi-m}} aḷavu vikaṟpaṅkaḷiṉāṉē

((uyirmey))

88

puḷḷiviṭ ṭavvoṭu muṉṉuru pākiyum
ēṉai yuyirō ṭurupu tirintum
mēviya mēviya vuyiraḷa pāyataṉ
vaṭivoḻit tiruvayiṟ peyaroṭum
oṟṟu muṉṉāy varumuyir meyyē.

e-ṉ, cārpeḻuttukkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam iṉṉum oruvakaiyāṉ uṇarttuvāṉeṭuttukkoṇṭār, avaṟṟuḷ iccūttiram uyir meyyāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): iccoṉṉapeṟṟiyiṟ ṟiriyātuvaruvatu uyirmey e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): eṉavē orumey akarattōṭu muṉṉaruruvēyākiyum, oḻinta uyirkaḷōṭu uruvuvēṟupaṭṭum varumeṉpatu pōntatu. pōtavē patiṉeṭṭumeyyum paṉṉiraṇṭuyirōṭuṅkūṭip paṉṉiru patiṉeṭṭu irunūṟṟorupattāṟāy mēṟcoṉṉa viriyāṉa vantavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.

va-ṟu. ka kā ki kī ku kū ke kē kai ko kō kau eṉa varum. oḻintaṉavum ivvāṟē oṭṭikkoḷka. uyirmey yeṉpatu ummait tokai.

{{pi-m}} muṉṉuruvu ṭuruvu tirintumuyiraḷavāy

((muṟṟāytam))

89

kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micaittē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē orumoḻiyiṭattuvarum muṟṟāytat tōṟṟamāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉar uyirōṭu kūṭiya valleḻuttu āṟaṉmicaiyē peṟappaṭum, āytavoṟṟu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): āytamāṉapuḷḷi āytappuḷḷi. `āytappuḷḷi' eṉṟār, itaṉaiyum oṟṟiṉpāṟ cārttutaṟkeṉka. oṟṟēl uyirēṟap peṟalvēṇṭumeṉiṉ, cārpeḻuttātaliṉ uyirēṟap peṟāteṉakkoḷka.

va-ṟu. eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu eṉavarum. aḵkāmai, veḵkāmai, aḵki, veḵki, aḵkō eṉpaṉa piṟavuyirkaḷōṭum vantaṉa. "vilaḵḵki vīṅkiru ḷōṭṭumē mātar, ilaḵḵku muttiṉiṉam" eṉa ivvāṟu kuṟiliṇaikkīḻum āytam varumālōveṉiṉ, aṉṉa iyalpākavē āytamāy niṟpaṉavalla. ōcai niṟaittaṟporuṭṭu oṟṟilvaḻi oṟṟākkutaliṉ, īṇṭu āytamum oṟṟāyvarumeṉak koḷka. irupaḵtu, aḵkaṭiya eṉap puṇarcciyiṭattu varum āytaṅkaḷai avvitiyiṟ kāṇka.

{{pi-m}} aḵkā, aḵtō vilaḵḵku

((uyiraḷapeṭai))

90

icaikeṭiṉ moḻimuta liṭaikaṭai nilaineṭil
aḷapeḻu mavaṟṟavaṟ ṟiṉakkuṟil kuṟiyē.

e-ṉ, vaittamuṟaiyāṉē uyiraḷapeṭait tōṟṟamāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): ceyyuṭkaḷ ōcai citaiya vantaviṭattu moḻimutaliṭai kaṭaiyeṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattum neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷtām iraṇṭu māttiraiyiṉ nīṇṭicaikkappeṟum. āṇṭu avaṟṟiṟku iṉamākiya kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ avvaḷaviṟkuk kuṟiyāyvantu niṟkum e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): eṉavē iraṇṭumāttiraiyāṉatu mūṉṟumāttiraiyāy nīṭalallatu iraṇṭeḻuttukkūṭi mūṉṟumāttiraiyā meṉpārai vilakkiṉārumām.

va-ṟu. vāakai, īikai, ūukam, pēekaṉ, taiiyal, tōokai, mauuval eṉavum; paṭāakai, parīikam, kaḻūumaṇi, parēeram, vaḷaiiyam, purōocai, maṉauukam eṉavam; kurāa, kurīi, kuḻūu, vilēe, viraii, ulōo, aṉauu eṉavum muṟaiyē mūviṭattum ēḻuneṭṭeḻuttum aḷapeḻuntaṉa. taṉinilaiyaḷapeṭai vēṇṭārāyiṉār, atu neṭṭeḻuttōreḻuttorumoḻiyāy mutaṉilai yaḷapeṭaiyāyattāṉ iṟutinilai yaḷapeṭaiyāyttāṉ aṭaṅkutaliṉeṉka. aḵtēl, "taṉinilai yiṭainilai yīṟeṉa, nālvakaip paṭūumaḷa pāyvakumiṭaṉē" eṉṉum muṉṉōrmatattōṭu muraṇumpiṟaveṉiṉ, "iruvar nūṟka moruciṟai toṭaṅkit, tiripuvē ṟuṭaiyatu puṭainū lākum" eṉpataṉālum māṟupāṭākāteṉka. `aiauiuc ceṟiya' eṉpataṉāṉ, aikārattukku ikaramum, aukārattukku ukaramum iṉakkuṟilāmeṉak koḷka. "eḻuttuk kuṟiyiṭa māttirai nāṉkum, vaḻukkātu vaikka vakuttu" eṉpavākaliṉ, īreḻuttukkūṭi aḷapāmeṉpār kuṟi kūṟiṟṟilarumām.

{{pi-m}} tōorai varīikam maṇimauuli kaṭāa, karāa kaḻūu milēe aṇauu

((oṟṟaḷapeṭai.))

91

ṅañaṇa namaṉa vayalaḷa vāytam
aḷapāṅ kuṟiliṇai kuṟiṟkī ḻiṭaikaṭai
mikalē yavaṟṟiṉ kuṟiyām vēṟē.

e-ṉ, oṟṟaḷapeṭaittōṟṟam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): iccollappaṭṭa pattoṟṟum āytamum kuṟiliṇaik kīḻum kuṟiṟkīḻum moḻiyiṭaiyiṉum īṟṟiṉumniṉṟu aḷapeṭukkum; avai iraṭṭittuniṟṟal avaṟṟiṟku aḷavu kuṟiyām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): āytam iṭaiyilaṉṟit tōṉṟāmaiyiṉ, iraṇṭiṭattum īṟṟil vantu aḷapeḻāteṉakkoḷka. iḵtu, `uyttuṇaravaippu' eṉṉum tantiravutti.

va-ṟu. araṅṅkam, muraññcu, muraṇṇṭu, parunntu, arummpu, muraṉṉṟu, kuravvvai, arayyyar, kurallkaḷ, tiraḷḷkaḷ, varaḵḵku e-m; maṅṅkalam, maññcu, maṇṇṇu, panntu, ammpu, miṉṉṉu, tevvvar, veyyyar, cellka, koḷḷka, eḵḵku eṉavum īriṭattum iṭainilaiyaḷapeḻuntaṉa. "araṇṇkoṇṭa vaṭutōḷiṉar", "valamm paṭṭa vāyvāḷar" "kalaṉṉkoṇṭa kaṉmārpar", "pakalllaṉṉa paṇpuraii", "tiraḷḷcēṉai celanīkki" eṉavum, kaṇṇṭaṇṇṇeṉak kaṇṭuṅkēṭṭum" (malaipaṭu. 352), "tammpaṭaiyait tānōkki", "miṉṉṉaṉṉa vēlviḷakki", "ivvvulakai yiṉitāṇṭu", "ceyytoḻiliṟ celanōkki", "kalllaṉṉa vaṉṉeñciṉ", "koḷḷḷeṉṟu koṭukkalarāy" eṉavum īriṭattum iṟutinilai aḷapeḻuntaṉa. īṇṭuk kāṭṭātoḻintaṉa arukiyaṉṟi vārāveṉakkoḷka. "icai keṭiṉ" eṉṟu uyiraḷapeṭaikku uraittataṉai īṇṭum varuvittuk koḷka. `vēṟu' eṉṟataṉāṉē, kuravai, arayar eṉpuḻi oṟṟukkaḷum, varaku eṉpuḻi āytamum, muṉ illaiyēyeṉiṉum, icaikeṭiṉ avaṟṟaiyuṇṭākkiyum, avaṟṟāṉum nirampāviṭiṉ avaṟṟai aḷapeṭuppittum ōcai niṟaikkappaṭum; eṉṉai? "ceyyuṭka ḷōcai citaiyuṅkā līraḷapu, maiyappā ṭiṉṟi yaṇaiyumā - maitīroṟ, ṟiṉṟiyuñ ceyyuṭ keṭiṉoṟṟai yuṇṭākku, kuṉṟumē loṟṟaḷapuṅ koḷ" eṉa māpurāṇamuṭaiyārum coṉṉārākaliṉ. "ampo raintuṭaiyya kāma ṉaiya ṉeṉṉa vantaṇa, ṉampu nīra rallar naṉku raṅku nīra rāyiṉum, taṅku ravvar tāṅko ṭuppi ṉeñcu nērntu tāḻvartām, poṅka ravvavalku lāre ṉappu kaṉṟu colliṉāṉ" (cīvaka. 1997.) itaṉuḷ oṟṟil vaḻi oṟṟu vantavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka. piṟavumaṉṉa.

{{pi-m}} vāḷvayavar kaviṉ ceyyyatoḻiṟ cēynōkki citaiyumēl pūtapurāṇamuṭaiyārum

((kuṟṟiyalikaram))

92

yakaram varakkuṟa ḷuttiri yikaramum
acaiccoṉ miyāvi ṉikaramuṅ kuṟiya.

e-ṉ, kuṟṟiyalikarattōṟṟam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): yakaram moḻikku mutalil vantaviṭattuk kuṟṟukaram tirintuvarum ikaramum miyāveṉṉum acainilaiyiṭaiccol ikaramum kuṟṟiyalikaramām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. nākiyātu, eḵkiyātu, varakiyātu, kokkiyātu, kuraṅkiyātu, toḷkiyātu eṉavum; kēṇmiyā, ceṉmiyā eṉavum varum. piṟavum ivvāṟē oṭṭikkoḷka.

{{pi-m}} teḷkiyātu

((kuṟṟiyalukaram))

93

neṭilō ṭāyta muyirvali meliyiṭai
toṭarmoḻi yiṟuti vaṉmaiyū rukaram
aḵkum piṟamēṟ ṟoṭaravum peṟumē.

e-ṉ, kuṟṟiyalukarattōṟṟam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): neṭṭeḻuttiṉpiṉṉum, āytamē uyirē valleḻuttē melleḻuttē iṭaiyeḻuttē eṉṟivaitoṭarnta moḻiyīṟṟiluniṉṟa kacaṭatapaṟakkaḷai ūrntuvantaukaram taṉmāttiraiyiṟ curuṅkivarum, neṭilmutalāṉa ivvāṟaṉmēlum oṉṟum palavum piṟaveḻuttut toṭaravumpeṟum e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): īroṟṟut toṭarntamoḻikaḷ vaṉṟoṭarumāy aṭaṅkumeṉakkoḷka.

va-ṟu. nāku, eḵku, varaku, kokku, kuraṅku, toḷku, e-m; ākātu, piṇṇākku, cuṇṇāmpu, irupaḵtu, āmaṇakku, ārkku, vāḻttu, moympu eṉavum varum. piṟavumaṉṉa. "neṭilē kuṟiliṇai kuṟiṉeṭi leṉṟivai, oṟṟoṭu varutaloṭu kuṟṟoṟ ṟiṟutiyeṉ, ṟēḻkuṟ ṟukarak kiṭaṉeṉa moḻipa" eṉṉum ivvēḻittuvarumeṉpaṉavum ivaṟṟuḷḷē yaṭaṅkumeṉpa. ivvāṟu ēḻiṭaṅkoḷvārkkuc cuṇṇāmpu, āmaṇakku, piṇṇākku mutalāṉavum āytantoṭarntaṉavum aṭaṅkāveṉṟaṟika. neṭilōṭu eṉap pirittōtiṉār, mūveḻuttuk kūṭiya toṭarmoḻi aṉmaiyiṉ.

{{pi-m}} ūrnta teḷku arakku, īrkku

((aikārakkuṟukkamum aukārakkuṟukkamum))

94

taṟcuṭ ṭaḷapoḻi yaimmū vaḻiyum
naiyu mauvu mutalaṟ ṟākum.

e-ṉ, aikāra aukārakkuṟukkaṅkaḷāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): taṉṉaiccollutaṟkaṇṇum aḷapeṭuttaṟkaṇṇumallātavaḻi vanta aikāram moḻimutal iṭai kaṭaiyeṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattum kuṟukum. ivvāṟuvarum aukāramum moḻimutaṟkaṇ avvāṟām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. aippaci, maippuṟam; maṭaiyaṉ, uṭaivāḷ; kuvaḷai, tavaḷai, tiṉai, paṉai e-m, mauval e-m varum. "kalleṉkauvai", "olleṉ pauvam" eṉpaṉa iṭaiyē kuṟaintavai yeṉpārumuḷar. avai kauvai, pauvameṉa mutalilē kuṟainta moḻikaḷ nilaimoḻikaḷōṭu puṇarntaṉa veṉa maṟukka. antau, attau eṉpaṉa kaṭaiyilēkuṟaintaṉaveṉiṉ, avai oru poruṭ ciṟappuṭaiyavāy naṭappaṉavallaveṉa maṟukka.

((makarakkuṟukkam))

95

ṇaṉamuṉṉum vaḵkāṉ micaiyumak kuṟukum.

((WFW)): ḷakara lakarantirinta ṇakaraṉakaraṅkaṇmuṉpum vakaramicaiyum niṉṟa makaram kuṟukum e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. "pacuppōlvār muṟpaṭṭāṟ pāṟpaṭṭa cāṉṟōr, mucuppōla muḷkān tiruppār - pacuttāṉ, veruḷiṉumellām veruḷumaḵtaṉṟi, maruḷiṉu mellā maruṇm", "emmoṭu tammaip porūuṅkāṟ poṉṉoṭu, kūviḷamottatum pōṉm", vāḻumvaḷavaṉ eṉavarum.

{{pi-m}} kūviḷampūttatum, kūviḷampōrttatum.

((āytakkuṟukkam))

((5. eḻuttiṉ uruvam))

97

tollai vaṭivaṉa vellā veḻuttumāṇ
ṭeytu mekara mokaramey puḷḷi.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē vaṭivāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. eri, eri; oḷi, oḷi; ulku, ulaku; maṇmakaḷ, maṇamakaḷ; kaṇmaṇi, kaṇamaṇi eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka. `āṇṭu' eṉṟa mikaiyāṉē, tātu, ētu eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattu āriyamoḻikaḷum, eṭṭu, koṭṭu eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattup potumoḻikaḷum, kuṉṟiyātu, nāṭiyātu, eṭṭiyāṇṭuḷatu eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattup puṇarmoḻipporuḷ vēṟupāṭukaḷum aṟitaṟporuṭṭuk kuṟṟukarak kuṟṟikaraṅkaḷukku mēṟpuḷḷikoṭuppārum uḷareṉak koḷka. uruvamuṟṟum.

((6. eḻuttiṉmāttirai))

98

mūṉṟuyi raḷapiraṇ ṭāneṭi loṉṟē
kuṟilō ṭaiyauk kuṟukka moṟṟaḷa
paraiyoṟ ṟiuk kuṟukka māytam
kālkuṟaṇ maḵkā ṉāyta māttirai.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē ellāveḻuttiṟkum aḷavāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): uyiraḷapeṭai mūṉṟum, neṭṭeḻuttu iraṇṭum kuṟṟeḻuttum aikārakkuṟukkamum aukārakkuṟukkamum oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyumeṉṉunāṉkum ōroṉṟu oṉṟum, meykaḷum kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷum āytamum ōroṉṟu araiyum makarakkuṟukkamum āytakkuṟukkamum ōroṉṟu kālum māttiraikaḷaḷavākavuṭaiyavām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): aṉṉavātal avaṟṟiṟku mēṟkāṭṭiya utāraṇaṅkaḷuṭ kāṇka.

{{pi-m}} oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyum ivaināṉkum. kālum aḷapāka.

99

 iyalpeḻu mānta rimainoṭi māttirai.

e-ṉ, mēl eḻuttiṟku aḷavu māttiraiyeṉṟār, īṇṭu ammāttiraiyāvatu iḵteṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): vikārappaṭātē iyalpāka eḻāniṉṟa māntaruṭaiya kaṇṇimaiyum kainnoṭiyum āṇṭuccoṉṉamāttiraikku aḷavām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): kaṭpulaṉākiya imaikkālamum cevippulaṉākiya noṭikkālamum karutik kōṭaṟku iraṇṭum ōtiṉāreṉka.

{{pi-m}} koṇṭaṟku

100

āviyu moṟṟu maḷaviṟan ticaittalum
mēvu micaiviḷi paṇṭamāṟ ṟātiyiṉ.

e-ṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuvitiyuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): uyirum oṟṟumeṉṉum irukūṟṟeḻuttum tamakkuc coṉṉa māttiraikaḷiṉ mikkicaikkavumpeṟum, icaiyum viḷiyum paṇṭamāṟṟum mutalāṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): `āti' eṉṟataṉāl, nāvalum muṟaiyīṭum pulampum kuṟippicai mutalāyiṉavuṅ koḷka. "kaḵṟeṉṉuṅ kallatarattam" eṉpatu kuṟippicai. ēṉaiya vantuḻikkāṇka. uyir paṉṉiraṇṭumāttiraiyum oṟṟup patiṉorumāttiraiyum nīḷumeṉṟār kantaruvanūluṭaiyār. avai vanta vaḻikkāṇka. nāval-pōrkkaḻaittal. māttiraimuṟṟum.

{{pi-m}} aḷapiṟantu kalleṉṉum 7. mutalnilai

101

paṉṉī ruyiruṅ kacatana pamavaya
ñaṅavī raintuyir meyyu moḻimutal.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttāvaṉa uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): iccollappaṭṭa irupattiraṇṭeḻuttum potuvakaiyāl moḻikkumutalām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. aṭai, āṭai, iṭai, īṭu, uṭai, ūṭal, eṭai, ēṭu, aiyam, oṭu, ōṭu, auviyam eṉavum; kaḷi, kāḷi, kiri, kīri, kuḷi, kūḷi, keṭal, kēṭu, kaitai, kovvai, kōvai, kauvai eṉavum; caḷi, cāral, cilai, cīlai, cuḷai, cūḷai, ceṟi, cēṟi, caiyam, cori, cōri, cauri eṉavum; tarai, tārai, tirai, tīrai, turai, tūrai, teri, tērai, taiyal, toyyil, tōyal tauvai eṉavum; narai, nārai, nirai, nīrmai, nurai, nūṟu, neri, nērmai, naivu, novvu, nōvu, nauvi eṉavum; paṭai, pāṭi, piṭi, pīṭu, puṭai, pūṭu, peṭai, pēṭu, paital, poytal, pōtal, pauvam eṉavum: malai, mālai, micai, mīcai, muḷai, mūḷai, meruḷ, mēṭu, maiyal, moḻi, mōḻai, mauval eṉavum varum. ivai muḻutum mutalāyiṉa. oḻintavai tattam ciṟappuvitiyiṟ kāṭṭutum. varimuṟaiyāṉvaiyātu itaṉuḷ mayaṅkavaittu vaḻakkup paṉmaipaṟṟiyeṉka. ṅakaram moḻikku mutalāmōveṉiṉ, "kacatapa ṅavvē yātiyu miṭaiyum, ṭaṟa viṭai ṇaṉaraḻa laḷaiṭai kaṭaiyē, ñaṅamaya vavvē mūṉṟiṭameṉpa" eṉa ṅakaram īriṭattum niṟku meṉṟār. āciriyar avinayaṉāru meṉakkoḷka.

{{pi-m}} kiḷi kaivai cuḷi cēri coṟi tūṟu tōrai noytu poykai pōrvai mikai, mīkai varicaimuṟaiyāṉ, varaṉmuṟaiyāṉ (1) kacatapa vāti yiṭaiyiṭa liṭaiyuḷa, vayanama ṅañamūṉ ṟiṭameṉa moḻipa. (2) kacatapa vāti yiṭaimī ṟavaṟṟuḷa, vayanama ñaṅamūṉ ṟiṭameṉa moḻipa. (3) kacatapa vāti yiṭaiyuḷa ṭaṟavuḷa, vayanama ñakkkaḷu mūṉṟiṭameṉpa. (4) kacatapa vāti yiṭaiyuṇa ṭavaṟṟuḷa, vayana maṅaña mūṉṟiṭameṉpa.

102

|u|ū|o|ō valavoṭu vammutal.

e-ṉ, potuvitimāṟṟic ciṟappuviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. (i-ṉ.) innāṉkumoḻinta uyirōṭuṅkūṭi vakaram moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. vaḷi, vāḷai, viḷi, vīḷai, veḷi, vēḷai, vaikal, vauvutal eṉavarum.

{{pi-m}} viḻi, viḷai. vīḻi, vīṇai.

103

aāuū oau yammutal. ituvumatu.

((WFW)): inta āṟuyirōṭuṅkūṭi yakaram moḻikkumutalām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. yavaṉar, yāṉai, yukam, yūkam, yōkam. yauvaṉam eṉa varum. mēlil oṭuvai varuvittuk koḷka.

{{pi-m}} yūki yōki

104

aāe ovvō ṭāku ñammutal ituvumatu.

((WFW)): innāluyirōṭuṅkūṭi ñakaram moḻikkumutalām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): (va-ṟu.) ñamaṉ, ñālam, ñekiḻntatu, ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉa varum.

{{pi-m}} ñamali ñāṉam ñoḷintatu

105

cuṭṭiyā vekara viṉāvaḻi yavvai
oṭṭi ṅavvu mutalā kummē. ituvumatu.

((WFW)): mūṉṟucuṭṭiṟkum yāvum ekaramumāya viṉākkaḷukkum vaḻiyē akarattōṭuṅkūṭi ṅakaram moḻikkumutalām. e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): (va-ṟu.) aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam, uṅṅaṉam, yāṅṅaṉam, eṅṅaṉam eṉavarum. ummai iṟantatutaḻīiyaeccavummai. "caricamaḻppuc caṭṭi caruku cavaṭi, caḷicakaṭu caṭṭai cavaḷi - cavicaraṭu, cantu cataṅkai caḻakkāti yīriṭattum, vantaṉavāṟ cammutalum vai". "kuṟṟiya lukara muṟaip peyar maruṅki, ṉoṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum" (moḻimarapu, 34)eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ivvāṟu kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻikku mutalāmeṉṟārālōveṉiṉ, "nuntaiyukaraṅ kuṟuki moḻimutaṟkaṇ, vanta teṉiṉuyīrmey yāmaṉaittum - cantik, kuyirmutalā vantaṇaiyu meyppuṇarcci yiṉṟi, mayalaṇaiyu meṉṟataṉai māṟṟu". ivaṟṟai viritturaittu vitiyum vilakkum aṟintakoḷka. moḻimutal muṟṟum.

{{pi-m}} cuṭṭuyā akarattōṭu ṅakaram vantaṉaiya

((8. īṟṟunilai))

106

āvi ñaṇanama ṉayarala vaḻaḷamey
cāyu mukara nālāṟu mīṟē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē moḻikku īṟṟeḻuttāvaṉa uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): iccollappaṭṭa uyirum meyyum kuṟṟukaramumākiya irupattunāleḻuttum moḻikku īṟāvaṉavām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. ā ī ū ē ai ō au eṉavum; uriñ, maṇ, porun, maram, poṉ, vēy, vēr, vēl, tev, vīḻ, vēḷ eṉavum, aḵku eṉavum varum. ivai potuvitiyāṟ pōntaṉa. ēṉaiya varuñcūttirattiṟ peṟutum. kuṟṟukaramum uyirāyaṅkumeṉiṉum puṇarcci mutalāṉa vēṟupāṭuṇmainōkki vēṟeṭuttōtiṉāreṉka.

{{pi-m}} eḵku

107

kuṟṟuyi raḷapi ṉīṟā mekaram
meyyo ṭēlā tonnav voṭā mau
kakara vakaramo ṭāku meṉpa.

e-ṉ, kuṟṟuyirkkup potuvitimāṟṟic ciṟappuvitiyum, ellāvuyirkkum potuvitiyiṉmēṟ ciṟappuvitiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): kuṟṟuyir aḷapiṉ īṟām e-tu, kuṟṟuyiraintum tāmē niṉṟu īṟākā, aḷapeṭuppuḻi īṟām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. āa, īi, ūu, ēe, aii, ōo, auu eṉa varum. ekaram meyyōṭu ēlātu e-tu, ekaram meykaḷōṭu kūṭi niṉṟu īṟākātu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): o navvoṭu ām e-tu, okaram nakaramoṉṟuṭaṉē kūṭi niṉṟu īṟām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. no eṉavarum. au kakara vakaramoṭu ākum e-tu, aukāram kakarattōṭum vakarattōṭuṅ kūṭiniṉṟu īṟām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. kau, vau eṉavarum. eṉpa e-tu, eṉṟucolluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): eṉavē, uyirkaḷ tāmēniṉṟum aḷapeṭuppuḻi niṉṟum īṟātalēyaṉṟi, okaram nakarattuṭaṉum, aukāram kakara vakaraṅkaḷuṭaṉum, ekara moḻinta ēṉaiya oṉpatuyirum tamakkēṟṟa meykaḷuṭaṉum kūṭiniṉṟu īṟām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): ekara okara aukāraṅkaḷai vitantōtiṉamaiyāṉ, ēṉai uyirkaḷ tamakkēṟṟa meykaḷōṭu īṟāmeṉpatu tāmēpōtarumeṉakkoḷka.

va-ṟu. vāḻka, mūṅkā, peruṅki, peruṅkī, paṅku, kū, eṅkē, eṅkai, eṅkō eṉavarum. ivvāṟē ēṟkum piṟameykaḷōṭum oṭṭikkoḷka. ēṟkumeṉpatu eṟṟāṟpeṟutumōveṉiṉ, cūttirattu eṭuttōtātu ñāpakavakaiyāṟ coṟṟamaiyāṟ peṟutum.

{{pi-m}} aukkakara pūṅkā (1) paruṅki (2) piruṅki, perukī peruku 6 (1) perukū (2) paṅkū.

108

niṉṟa neṟiyē yuyirmey mutalīṟē.

e-ṉ, uyirmey mutalum īṟumāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): oṟṟumuṉṉum, uyir piṉṉumāy niṟṟaliṉ uyirmeyyāṉatu meymutal moḻiyeṉṉum uyirīṟṟumoḻiyeṉṟumāvataṉṟi uyirmeymutaleṉṉam uyirmey īṟeṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭātu e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): īṟu muṟṟum.

((9. iṭainilai))

109

kacatapa voḻittavī rēḻaṉ kūṭṭam
meymmayak kuṭaṉilai raḻavoḻit tīreṭ
ṭākumiv virupāṉ mayakku moḻiyiṭai
mēvu muyirmey mayakkaḷa viṉṟē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē iṭainilaiyāmāṟu uṇarttuvāṉeṭuttukkoṇṭār, avaṟṟuḷ iccūttiram, potuvākaiyāl meymmayakkamum uṭaṉilaimayakkamum āmāṟum avaṟṟateṇṇum avainiṟkumiṭamum āmāṟum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): mey patiṉeṭṭuḷḷum kacatapaveṉṉum nāṉkum oḻittuniṉṟa patiṉālumeyyum oṉṟaṉpiṉoṉṟu māṟivantu oṉṟuvatu meymmayakkamām; rakara ḻakaramoḻittuniṉṟa patiṉāṟu meyyum tammuṉṉart tāmvantoṉṟuvatu uṭaṉilaimayakkamām. iccollappaṭṭa irukūṟṟu muppatumayakkum moḻiyiṭaiyilēyām. uyirmeymayakkam iṉṉataṉpiṉṉar iṉṉatāmeṉṉum varaiyaṟaiyillai e-ṟu.

((VIRI)):

{{pi-m}} meynnilaimayakkamum avaṟṟiṟkeṇṇum

((meymmayakkam))

110

ṅammuṉ kavvām vammuṉ yavvē.

e-ṉ, ṅakara vakara meymmayakkam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. nilam, nilaṉ; pulam, pulaṉ; kalam, kalaṉ; valam, valaṉ; mukam, mukaṉ; akam, akaṉ eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa uṟaḻntaṉa; `uṟaḻā naṭappaṉavuḷa' eṉavē, uṟaḻātaṉa perumpāṉmaiyavāmeṉak koḷka. avai: vaṭṭam, paṭṭam, kuṭṭam, māṭam, kūṭam, kaṭām, paṭām, kaṭakam, caṭakam, nukam, makam, āram, pūram, uttaram, vīkkam, nōkkam, ūkkam eṉṟāṟṟoṭakkattaṉa. iṉi, ivviti ivvāṟaṉṟi, "makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta, ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi yoṉpaḵ teṉpa, pukaraṟak kiḷanta vaḵṟiṇaimēṉa" (tol. moḻimarapu, 49) eṉṟu ekiṉ, cekiṉ, eyiṉ, vayiṉ, kuyiṉ, aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ, pulāṉ, kaṭāṉ eṉa varum oṉpatum mayaṅkātaṉaveṉak koḷḷiṉ, paliyaṉ, valiyaṉ, vayāṉ, kayāṉ, alavaṉ, kalavaṉ, kaluḻaṉ, maṟaiyaṉ, cekilaṉ mutalāyiṉavum mayaṅkappeṟāveṉa maṟukka.

{{pi-m}} cakaṭam vicākam ōkkam aḻāṉ, puḻāṉ vayāṉ payiṉ (1) vayiṉ, (2) valiyāṉ kaḻāṉ ikalaṉ

((eḻuttiṉ cāriyai))

125

meyka ḷakaramu neṭṭuyir kāramum
aiau kāṉu mirumaik kuṟiliv
viraṇṭoṭu karamumāñ cāriyai peṟumpiṟa.

e-ṉ, atikarittamuṟaiyāṉē eḻuttiṉpakutiyāṉa eṇ mutaṟpattumuṇartti avveḻuttu vaḻaṅkutaṟkuvarum cāriyai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. ka ṅa ca ña .... ṟa ṉa; ākāram īkāram ūkāram ēkāram aikāram ōkāram aukāram; aikāṉ aukāṉ; akāram ikāram ukāram ekāram okāram kakāram ṅakāram... ṟakāram ṉakāram, aḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ, akaram ikaram ukaram ekaram okaram kakaram ... ṉakaram eṉavarum. `piṟa' eṉṟamikaiyāṉē kāṉvaruṅkālai āytamum, āṉa eṉa mutalāṉavuṅ koḷka.

{{pi-m}} ai aukkāṉum (1) āṉum ēṉum ōṉum ammutalāṉavum (2) āṉum ēṉum ammutalāṉavum (3) āṉum ēṉavum āṉamutalāṉavum

126

moḻiyāyt toṭariṉu muṉṉaṉait teḻuttē.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. maṟu maṟṟu; maṟṟai eṉavum, "kaṟka kacaṭaṟak kaṟpavai kaṟṟapi, ṉiṟka vataṟkut taka" (kuṟaḷ. 391) eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟuḷ avvāṟātal kāṇka.

{{pi-m}} maṉṟu māṟai

((mutalāvatu eḻuttiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.))

((iraṇṭāvatu pataviyal))

127

eḻuttē taṉittun toṭarntum poruṭariṟ
patamā matupakāp patampaku patameṉa
irupā lāki yiyalu meṉpa.

eṉpatu cūttiram. ivvōttu eṉṉutaliyatōveṉiṉ, ōttunutaliyatum, ōttiṉatupeyaruraippavē viḷaṅkum. āyiṉ, ivvōttu eṉṉapeyarttōveṉiṉ, pataṅkaḷiṉatu iyalpu uṇarttiṟṟātalāṟ pataviyaleṉṉum peyarttu. mēlōttiṉōṭu itaṟku iyaipu eṉṉaiyōveṉiṉ, mēl eḻuttilakkaṇam paṉṉirupakutiyāṉ uṇarttappaṭumeṉṟavaṟṟuḷ eḻuttiṟkēyuriya pattilakkaṇamum pōṉavōttiṉāṉuṇartti, avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉiṉṟatu avveḻuttiṉāṉākiya patamākaliṉ, ataṉai īṇṭuṇarttutaliṉ, iyaipuṭaitteṉka. ivvōttiṉuḷ ittalaic cūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, potuvakaiyāṟ patamāmāṟum ataṉ pakutiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): mēṟcoṉṉa eḻuttuttāṉē taṉiyē niṉṟum toṭarntu niṉṟum poruḷai viḷakkumēyeṉiṉ atu patameṉpatām; atu pakāp patamum pakupatamumeṉa irupakutiyāy naṭakkumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): eṉavē ellāmoḻiyum ōreḻuttuppatam toṭareḻuttuppatameṉavum pakāppatam pakupatameṉavum ivviraṇṭāy aṭaṅkumeṉpatām. iṟiñi, miṟiñi mutalāyiṉa toṭarntaṉavēṉum poruḷtārāvākaliṟ patamākāveṉpatāyiṟṟu. patameṉiṉum moḻiyeṉiṉum colleṉiṉum okkum. "taṉṉai yuṇartti ṉeḻuttām piṟaporuḷaic, cuṭṭutaṟ kaṇṇēyāñ col" eṉpatu avinayappuṟanaṭai

128

uyirmavi lāṟun tapanavi laintum
kavacavi ṉālum yavvi loṉṟum
āku neṭiṉotu vāṅkuṟi liraṇṭō
ṭōreḻut tiyalpata māṟēḻ ciṟappiṉa.

e-ṉ, mēl eḻuttut taṉiyē niṉṟum patamāmeṉṟār, avai ivai yeṉpatūum ittuṇaiveṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): uyirilum makarattilum avvāṟum, tavvilum pavvilum navvilum aivaintum, kavvilum vavvilum cavvilum nannāṉkum, yavvil oṉṟum āku neṭṭeḻuttum, novvum tuvvumām kuṟṟeḻuttiraṇṭumāka ōreḻuttorumoḻi nāṟpattiraṇṭum ciṟappuḷavām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. ā, ī, ē, ai, o, au; mā, mī, mū, mai, mō: tā, tī, tū, tē, tai; pā, pū, pē, pai, pō; nā, nī, nē, nai, nō; kā, kū, kai, kō; vā, vī, vē, vai; cā, cī, cē, cō; yā; no, tu eṉavarum. ivai ciṟappuḷaveṉavē, ciṟappillaṉavum cilavuḷavāyiṉa. avai āṟāmuyirum, pakaravīkāramum, ci, cū, cai, kau, vau eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉavumeṉakkoḷka. `uyir mavilāṟum' eṉṟataṉai, avvavvarukkaṅkaḷi leṉak koḷka.

129

pakāppata mēḻum pakupata moṉpatum
eḻuttī ṟākat toṭaru meṉpa.

e-ṉ, mēl niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē toṭareḻuttuppatattiṟku varaiyaṟaiyuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): pakāppatam iraṇṭeḻuttumutal ēḻeḻuttīṟākavum pakupatam iraṇṭeḻuttu mutal oṉpateḻuttīṟākavum toṭarumeṉpar pulavar e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. aṇi, aṟam, akalam, aruppam, taruppaṇam, uttiraṭṭāti eṉap pakāppatam toṭarntavāṟu. kūṉi, kūṉaṉ, kuḻaiyaṉ, poruppaṉ, ampalavaṉ, araṅkattāṉ, attikōcattāṉ, uttiraṭṭātiyāṉ eṉap pakupatan toṭarntavāṟu. kaṅkaikoṇṭa cōḻapuram, iratanūpura cakkaravāḷam (cūḷā. iratanūpura. 12), pāṇṭiyaṉ palyākacālai mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattu oṭṭuppeyarkku varaiyaṟaiyillai yeṉakkoḷka.

130

pakuppāṟ payaṉaṟ ṟiṭukuṟi yāki
muṉṉē yoṉṟāy muṭintiyal kiṉṟa
peyarviṉai yiṭaiyuri nāṉkum pakāppatam.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē pakāppatamāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): oṉṟāyniṉṟu oruporuḷ tarumoḻiyaip palavākappirittāl ataṉāl varuvatōrpayaṉaṟṟu iṭukuṟiyāyt toṉṟutoṭṭu oru piṇṭamāy naṭakkiṉṟa peyarccollum viṉaiccollum iṭaiccollum uriccollumeṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟavai pakāppatam e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. nampi, naṅkai, cāttaṉ, cātti, koṟṟaṉ, koṟṟi, tantai, tāy, yāṉai, kutirai, uyir, uṭampu, malai, veṟpu, tī, nīr, oṉṟu, kaḻañcu, kalam e-m; vāṉ, nilam, akam, puṟam e-m; yāṇṭu, vēṉil, paṅkuṉi, ātirai, pakal, irā, kālai, mālai, nerunal, iṉṟu e-m; kaṇ, cevi, mūkku, kai, kāl, kōṭu, ciṉai, taḷir, pū, kāy, kaṉi e-m; cemmai, veṇmai, karumai, neṭumai, kuṟumai, iṉmai, uṇmai e-m; uṇal, tiṉal, celavu, varavu, kūttu, pāṭṭu e-m varum ittoṭakkattup poruḷāti yaṟuvakaipporuṭ peyarkaḷellām peyarppakāppatam. uṇ, tiṉ, naṭa, kiṭa, pō, vā, urai, iru, cel eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa viṉaippakāppatam. maṉ, maṟṟu, til, amma, kol, miyā, ika, mō, mati eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa iṭaippakāppātam. cāla, uṟu, tava, naṉi, kaḻi, kali, oli, muḻakku eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa urippakāppatam. peyaruḷḷum cāttaiyuṭaiyāṉ cāttaṉ, koṟṟaiyuṭaiyāṉ koṟṟaṉ, kūttaiyuṭaiyāṉ kūttaṉeṉṟu ivvāṟu poruḷpaṭiṉ avai pakāppatamākā; iṭukuṟi māttiraiyāy niṟkilē pakāppatamāvateṉak koḷka. avaṉ, avaḷ, avar eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa īṟu pakukka vēṟupālkāṭṭaliṟ pakupatamām piṟaveṉiṉ, avai oṉṟāy niṉṟu oruporuḷāvatallatu īṟupirittāṟ pakuti vēṟu poruḷpaṭāmaiyiṉ, avaṟṟiṟkup pakutivikutit taṉmaiyiṉmaiyiṉ ākāveṉka. iṉi, col nāṉkākavēṇṭiyatu eṉṉaiyōveṉiṉ, peyarccol poruḷai viḷakkutalāṉum, viṉaiccol poruḷatutoḻilai viḷakkutalāṉum, iṭaiccoṟkaḷ, ivviraṇṭiṟkum vikutiyurupukaḷākiyum, vēṟṟumai uvamai cāriyaiyurupukaḷākiyum, cila peyarākiyum, cila viṉaiyākiyum, terinilai, tēṟṟam, aiyam, muṟṟu, eṇ, ciṟappu, etirmaṟai, eccam, viṉā, viḻaivu, oḻiyicai, pirippu, kaḻivu, ākkam, icainiṟai, acainilai, kuṟippu mutalāṉa poruṇmai viḷakkutalāṉum, uriccol, peyarāmeṉiṉum poruḷai viḷakkalum perumpāṉmaiyum urupēṟṟalumiṉṟip poruṭkuṇattaiyē viḷakkutalāṉum, cila viṉai pōl varutalāṉum colliṟku innāṟpakutiyum ivaṭkiṭappum vēṇṭumeṉavē koḷka. aṉṟiyum, "peyariṉum viṉaiyiṉu moḻimuta laṭaṅkum" eṉṟa mikatteḷikēḷvi akattiyaṉārum, "colleṉappaṭupa peyarē viṉaiyeṉ, ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa vaṟintici ṉōrē" (peyariyal, 4) eṉṟa olkāpperumait tolkāppayiṉārum, "avaitām, peyarccoleṉṟā toḻiṟco leṉṟā, iraṇṭaṉ pālā yaṭaṅkumaṉ payiṉṟē" eṉṟa aḷavaṟupulamai avinayaṉārum piṉpum ippakutiyum immuṟaiyum vēṇṭiṉāreṉakkoḷka.

poruḷiṭaṅ kālañ ciṉaikuṇan toḻiliṉ
varupeyar poḻutukoḷ viṉaipaku patamē.

e-ṉ, pakupatamāvaṉa uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): poruḷē iṭaṉē kālamē uṟuppē paṇpē toḻilē eṉṟivvāṟum kāraṇamākavarum peyarccoṟkaḷum, kālampaṟṟivarum viṉaiccoṟkaḷum pakupataṅkaḷām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): poruṇmutaṉmūṉṟum mutalum, ciṉaimutaṉmūṉṟum ciṉaiyumāyaṭaṅkumeṉpataṟivittaṟku immuṟai vaittāreṉka. `poruḷiṭaṅ kālañ ciṉaikuṇan toḻiliṉ, varupeyar poḻutukoḷviṉai' eṉavē, ivaitāmaṉṟi ivai kāraṇamākap piṟavaṟṟiṟkām peyarviṉaikaḷē pakupatamāvaṉaveṉakkoḷka. poruḷāti ((*1:VAR)) ēḻiṉaiyuntoṭarntu oṉṟāy niṉṟamoḻiyaip pakuti vikuti iṭainilaiyeṉap pirittuk kāraṇaṅkāṭṭi mīṭṭuṅkūṭṭi oru moḻiyākavē ākkikkōṭaliṟ pakupatameṉak kāraṇappeyarāmeṉka; utāraṇam mēṟkāṭṭutum, peyarppakupatattōṭum, ākupeyarppatattōṭum tokaippatattōṭum ((*2:VAR)) viṉaippeyarōṭum muṟṟuviṉaippatattōṭum vēṟṟumaiyātōveṉiṉ, peyarppakupatam, pirittāl, pakuti pakāppatamum vikuti vēṟuporuḷil iṭaiccollumāyt toṭarntuniṉṟu poruḷai viḷakkum; avai:- ūraṉ, veṟpaṉ, villi, vāḷiyeṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa. ākupeyarppatamum kāraṇattiṉāṉ āmēyeṉiṉum vikutiyiṉṟip pakutiyāṉa pakāppatantāṉē piṟitum oruporuḷai viḷakkum. avai: teṅku, kaṭu, puḷi, kuḻippāṭi, cīṉam, ēṟu, kuttu, nāḻi eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa. tokaip patam iraṇṭum palavum pakāppatamum pakupatamum nilaimoḻi varumoḻiyāyt toṭarntu iraṇṭumutalāya poruḷ tōṉṟaniṟkum; avai: yāṉaikkōṭu, kolyāṉai, karuṅkutirai eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa. oru toḻiṟcol eṭṭuvēṟṟumaiyurupumēṟkiṉ ((*3:VAR)) viṉaippeyarām; aṉṟit taṉṉeccamāṉa peyarkoṇṭumuṭiyiṉ muṟṟuviṉaic collām. utāraṇam uṇṭāṉ eṉpatu; uṇṭāṉai uṇṭāṉoṭu eṉavum, uṇṭāṉcāttaṉ uṇṭāṉtēvaṉeṉavum varum. aṉṟiyum eṭuttalōcaiyāṟcolla viṉaippeyarām; paṭuttalōcaiyāṟ colla muṟṟuviṉaippatamāmeṉak koḷka.

īṟu pōta liṭaiyukara miyyātal
āti nīṭa laṭiyakara maiyātal
taṉṉoṟ ṟiraṭṭaṉ muṉṉiṉṟa meytirital
iṉamika liṉaiyavum paṇpiṟ kiyalpē.

e-ṉ, appaṇpuppatattiṟkuriya cilaviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): paṇpuppatattiṉ iṟutikeṭutalum, iṭaiyiṉiṉṟa ukaram ikaramātalum, mutaliṉiṉṟa uyirkaḷ nīṭalum, mutaliṉiṉṟa akaram aikāramātalum, iṭaiyē taṉṉoṟṟumikutalum. muṉpu niṉṟamey tiritalum, varumoḻimutalukku iṉaveḻuttumikutalum, ivai pōlvaṉa piṟavum muṟaimai e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): īṟṟucceykaiyai mutaliṟ kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ikkēṭu orutalaiyeṉpatūum, keṭuvaḻi īṟṟuyir keṭutalum, īṟṟuyirmey keṭutalum, īṟṟuyirmeyyoṭum īṟṟayaluyir keṭutalum, iṉaveḻuttu varumoḻikkaṇṇateṉpatūum, ivvitiyellām oṉṟaṟkalla veṉpatūum koḷka.

va-ṟu. kariyaṉ, periyaṉ, neṭiyaṉ, ciṟiyaṉeṉpaṉa īṟṟuyir meykeṭṭum iṭaiukaram ikaramākiyum vantaṉa. karuṅkutirai, ceṅkōleṉpaṉa īṟṟuyirum īṟṟuyirmeyyum muṟaiyē keṭṭu oṟṟuttirintaṉa. kārā, pērā, pāciḻai (cīvaka. 586), pācaṭai (kuṟun. 9) eṉpaṉa īṟṟuyir meyyum īṟṟayaluyiruṅkeṭṭu ātinīṇṭaṉa. paiṅkaṇ, paintār eṉpaṉa īṟṟuyirmeyyum īṟṟayaluyiruṅkeṭṭu ātiniṉṟa akaram aikāramāy oṟṟuttirintaṉa. veṟṟilai, ciṟṟāṉai, kuṟṟaṭi, kuṟṟuyir, neṭṭilai, neṭṭeḻuttu eṉpaṉa īṟṟuyir meyyum īṟṟayaluyirum keṭṭu iṭaiyē taṉṉoṟṟumikkaṉa. cētāmpal eṉpatu īṟṟuyirmey keṭṭu muṉpuḷatāyniṉṟa mey tirintu āti nīṇṭatu; cēṅkaṉṟu (cemmai+kaṉṟu) eṉpatu varumoḻikkiṉamāṉa melleḻuttu mikkatu. karuṅkutirai mutalāyiṉa pakupatamallavēṉum paṇpatikārappaṭṭamaiyāṟ patappuṇarccikkum īṇṭē coṉṉāreṉakkoḷka. `iṉaiyavum' eṉṟataṉāṟ paṇpiṟkuvarumuṭipu piṟavumuḷavēl avaiyum īṇṭē koḷka.

e-ṉ, ceyvi ceyvippiyeṉṉum ēvalviṉaippakāppataṅkaḷ uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): ceyyeṉṉum ēvalviṉaiyiṉ piṉpu vi pi eṉṉum iraṇṭiṉuḷ oṉṟuvariṟ ceyviyeṉṉum poruḷaip peṟum. ivaiyiraṇṭum oruṅkuvariṉum oṉṟē iṇaintuvariṉum ēvaṉ mēlēval tōṉṟa mūvarāvāṉoru karuttaṉaik kāṭṭum e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. naṭappi, varuvi, maṭivi, cīppi, viṭuvi, kūvuvi, ēvuvi, vaivi, novvuvi, pōvuvi, vauvuvi, uriñuvi, uṇpi, porunuvi, tirumuvi, tiṉpi, tēyvi, tēyppi, pārppi, celvi, vavvuvi, vāḻvi, kēṭpi, aḵkuvi eṉac ceyvi yeṉṉum ēval viṉaippakāppatam vantavāṟu. naṭattuvippi, varuvippi, maṭivippi eṉavum, naṭappippi, kaṟpippi eṉavum iraṇṭum iṇaintum oṉṟēyiṇaintum ceyvippiyeṉṉum ēvaṉmēl ēvaṟpakāppatam vantavāṟu. ivaṟṟāṟ pakupatam varumāṟu:- naṭattuvittāṉ, naṭattuvittāḷ, naṭattuvittār eṉavum, naṭappittāṉ, naṭappittāḷ, naṭappittār eṉavum varum. ivvāṟē ellāttiṇaipāl iṭam kālaṅkaḷtōṟu moṭṭic ceyviyeṉṉum ēvaṟkup pakupatam vantavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka. naṭattuvippittāṉ, naṭattuvippittāḷ, naṭattuvippittāreṉavum, naṭappippittāṉ, naṭappippittāḷ, naṭappippittār; kaṟpippittāṉ, kaṟpippittāḷ, kaṟpippittār eṉavum ellāttiṇaipāliṭaṅkālaṅkaḷ tōṟumoṭṭic ceyvippiyeṉṉum irumaṭiyēvaṟkup pakupatam vantavāṟu kāṇka. ivaiyellām ēṟṟapeṟṟi koḷka.

{{pi-m}} ceyvi, ceypi, ceyvippi ivviraṇṭum oruṅkuvariṉ ēvalmēl ēvaltōṉṟi mūvarāvārkkorukaruttaṉai cīyppi aḵkupi

138

viḷampiya pakutivē ṟātalum vitiyē.

e-ṉ, pakutikkuriyatōr viti yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): mēṟcoṉṉa ēṉaivakaip pakutikaḷuḷḷum cila poruḷāṉ vēṟupaṭṭum cila mikkum cila tirintum cila īṟukeṭṭum niṟkaiyum kuṟṟamākā e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): naṭa vā maṭi cī eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattu viṉaippakāppataṅkaḷ muṉṉilaip poruḷavēṉum ēṉaiyiṭaṅkaḷukkum tirintum poruḷēṟkum.

va-ṟu. vā eṉpakuti, vantāṉ varukiṉṟā ṉeṉpuḻi makaram mikkum tirintum varum. koḷḷeṉpakuti, koṇṭāṉ, kōṭu, kōṭum eṉpuḻi ḷakāram ṇakāramākiyum keṭṭum āti nīṇṭum vantatu. kol, cel, vel eṉṉum pakutikaḷ koṉṟāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, veṉṟāṉ; kōṟu kōṟum, cēṟu cēṟum, vēṟu vēṟum eṉpuḻi lakāram ṉakāramākiyum keṭṭum āti nīṇṭum vantaṉa. valaicci, pulaicci, kuyatti, kaṇavāṭṭi eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattup peyarppakupataṅkaḷellām valaimai, pulaimai mutalāṉa peyarppakāppatattu īṟukeṭṭu i ēṟṟu vantaṉa. ācuvi, caṉṉāli, ēṉāti, cēmāṟi, kaivāri, tiruvili, parivili, aṟivili, poṟiyili, nāḷōti, nūlōti eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉavum īṟukeṭṭaṉa. piṟavum ituvē nilaṉākap pakutikku varuvaṉavellām varuvittuk koḷka.

aṉ āṉ aḷ āḷ ar ār pammār
aā kuṭutuṟu eṉēṉ alaṉ
amām emēm omo ṭummūr
kaṭataṟa ai āy immiṉ irīr
5 īyar kayavu meṉpaṉavum piṟavum
viṉaiyiṉ vikuti peyariṉuñ cilavē.

e-ṉ, pakupataṅkaṭku vikutiyāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): iccoṉṉa muppattēḻum ivaipōlvaṉa piṟavum viṉaippakupatattiṉkaṇ vikutiyām; peyarppakupatattiṉ kaṇṇum cila vikutiyām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. naṭantaṉaṉ, naṭantāṉ, naṭantaṉaḷ, naṭantāḷ, naṭantaṉar, naṭantār, naṭappa, naṭamār, naṭantaṉa, naṭavā, naṭakku, uṇṭu, kuṇṭukaṭṭu, naṭantu, naṭantatu, vēṟu, kūyiṟṟu, naṭantaṉeṉ, naṭantēṉ, naṭappāl, naṭappaṉ, naṭappam, naṭappām, naṭappem, naṭappēm, naṭappōm, naṭakkum, uṇṭum, naṭantum, ceṉṟum, naṭantaṉai, naṭantāy, naṭatti, naṭamiṉ, naṭantaṉir, naṭantīr, nilīyar, naṭakka, vāḻiya, naṭavum eṉa viṉaikkaṇ muppattēḻum viṉaimuṟṟu vikutiyāyiṉa. piṟavaṟṟoṭum ivvāṟē ēṟpikka. piṟavum eṉṟataṉāl, peyareccaviṉaiyecca vikutikaḷ vāypāṭukaḷum aṟintu pakuti vikutiyākki iṭainilaiyum vēṇṭuḻittantu muṭikka. naṭantavaṉ naṭantāṉ eṉat toḻiṟpeyarkkum ivvāṟē ēṟpaṉavaṟintu oṭṭikkoḷka. villaṉ, villāṉ, vaḷaiyal, vaḷaiyāḷ, ūrar, ūrār, villi, vāḷi, uruvili, tiruvili, poṟiyili, ceviyili, araci, pārppaṉi, ceṭṭicci, uḻatti, kiḻatti, kaṇavāṭṭi, vaṇṇātti, kātaṟai, ceviyaṟai eṉap peyarkkaṇ vikutiyāyiṉa. piṟavum vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟē palaviṉai peyarkaṭkuvaruvaṉavum koḷka. īṇṭup patamuṭittaṟkup pakuti vikuti māttiraiyē kāṭṭiyatu; tiṇaipāliṭam kālaṅkaṭkuriyavāmāṟu collatikārattuṭ peyarviṉaiyōttukkaḷuṭ collutum.

{{pi-m}} naṭattum cēṟum mūṉṟiṭattum viṉaimuṟṟu vikutikaḷāṉa vāypāṭukaḷum pārppiṉi viṉaippeyarkaṭkum tiṇaipāliṭaṅkaṭku

ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇa miyampaliṟ
pakuti vikuti pakuttiṭai niṉṟatai
viṉaippeya ralpeyark kiṭainilai yeṉalē.

((WFW)): muṟkālattiṟkaṇṭa ilakkiyaṅkaṭkē ilakkaṇam iyampaliṉ, īṇṭu muṭikkaluṟum patattiṉ pakutiyaiyum vikutiyaiyum aṟintu, vēṟu pirittāl iṭaiyilēniṟpatuyātu, ataṉai viṉaippeyarallāta peyarkku iṭainilaiyeṉak koḷḷavēṇṭum e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. vāṉavaṉ, mīṉavaṉ, villavaṉ, ellavaṉ, pakalavaṉ, katiravaṉ, kariyavaṉ, ceyyavaṉ, veyyavaṉ, kuṟiyavaṉ, neṭiyavaṉ, putiyavaṉ, paḻaiyavaṉ, ciṟiyavaṉ, periyavaṉ eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa, `a' eṉṉum iṭainilaipeṟṟaṉa. cēramāṉ, kaṭṭimāṉ eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa makara iṭainilaipeṟṟaṉa. valaicci, pulaicci eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa cakaraiṭainilai peṟṟaṉa. pulaitti, vaṇṇātti eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa takaraviṭainilaipeṟṟaṉa. veḷḷāṭṭi, malaiyāṭṭi mutalāyiṉa ṭakaraviṭainilaipeṟṟaṉa. kaṇakkicci, taccicci mutalāyiṉa ic eṉṉum iṭainilai peṟṟaṉa. piṟavum ivvāṟē aṟintukoḷka. canti vakaiyāṉum potuccāriyaivakaiyāṉum muṭiyāvaḻi ivvāṟuvaruvaṉa iṭainilaiyeṉak koḷka. viṉaippeyarkku viṉaippatattiṟkuc coṉṉavaiyē iṭainilaiyeṉakkoḷka.

{{pi-m}} kāṭṭaliṉ peyarkaṭku pulatti aṟintuvaruvikka coṉṉavaiyēkoḷka.

((WFW)): pakaravakaraṅkaḷāṉa iraṇṭoṟṟum, mūviṭattuvarum aimpālkaḷilum etirkālaṅkāṭṭum viṉaippakupata iṭainilaikaḷām. im mukkālamuṅ kāṭṭum iṭainilaikaḷaic cila viṉaimoḻikaḷ ēlāvām e-ṟu.

144

ṟavvo ṭukara vummainikaḻ pallavum
tavvo ṭiṟappu metirvum ṭavvoṭu
kaḻivuṅ kavvō ṭetirvumiṉ ṉēval
viyaṅkō ḷimmā retirvum pāntam
celavoṭu varavuñ ceyyunikaḻ vetirvum
etirmaṟai mummaiyu mēṟku mīṅkē.

e-ṉ, mēl viṉaikkaṭ kālaṅkāṭṭuvaṉa iṭainilaikaḷeṉṟum cilaviṉaimoḻikaḷ avviṭainilaikaḷai ēlāveṉṟum pōtantār, avvēlātaṉa ivaiyeṉpatūum avaṟṟāṟpeṟuṅkālamum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): ṟakarattoṭu kūṭivanta ukaravīṟum ummīṟumāṉa viṉaiccoṟkaḷ kaḻintakālamum varuṅkālamum, takarattoṭukūṭi niṉṟa avviraṇṭīṟṟuviṉaiyum iṟantakālamum etirkālamum ṭakarattoṭukūṭi niṉṟa avviraṇṭīṟṟuviṉaiyum iṟantakālamum, kakarattoṭukūṭiya avviraṇṭīṟṟuviṉaiyum etirkālamum, miṉṉīṟum ēvaṟporuṇmaiyiṉ varum aṉaittīṟukaḷum viyaṅkōṭporuḷavum ikaravīṟum mārīṟumāṉa viṉaikaḷ etirkālamum, pakaravīṟṟuviṉai iṟantakālamum etirkālamum, ceyyumeṉṉum vāyppāṭṭuviṉaikaḷ nikaḻkālamum etirkālamum, etirmaṟaiviṉai mukkālamum kāṭṭuvaṉavām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. ceṉṟu ceṉṟum, cēṟu cēṟum, vantu vantum, varutu varutum, uṇṭu uṇṭum, uṇku uṇkum, uṇmiṉ, uṇ, uṇka, vāḻiya, vāḻiyar, cēṟi, uṇmār, uṇpa, uṇṇum, uṇṇāṉ eṉa muṟaiyē kāṇka. `īṅku' eṉṟamikaiyāṉ, īṇṭōtiyakālattiṟ piṟaḻntu varuvaṉa uḷavāyiṉa, avaiyum, iṭainilaiyēlāp peyarecca viṉaiyeccaṅkaḷum aṟintu muṭiyumāṟṟāṟ pakutivikutipaṇṇi muṭikka.

iṉi, ivvāṟṟāṟ pakupatamuṭippuḻi, malaiyaṉeṉṉum pakupatamuṭiyumāṟāvatu:- malaiyaiyuṭaiyaṉeṉṉum poruṇmaitōṉṟa, "tattam ..pakutiyākum" eṉpataṉāṉē malaiyeṉṉum poruṭpakutiyai mutalilvaittu ataṉmītē, "aṉ āṉ aḷ āḷ" eṉpataṉāṉē aṉṉeṉṉum vikutiyainiṟuvi, "i ī yaivaḻi yavvum" eṉpataṉāṉē iṭaiyē yakaravuṭampaṭu meyyai varuvittu, "uṭaṉmē luyirvan toṉṟuvatiyalpē" eṉpataṉāṉē yakarattiṉmēlē vikuti akarattai yēṟṟi muṭikka. malaiyateṉpataṟkut tuvveṉṉumvikutimai niṟuvi, "aṉṉā ṉiṉṉa laṟṟiṟṟu" eṉpataṉāṉē akaraccāriyaiyai iṭaiyē tantu, muṉpōl uṭampaṭumeyyai varuvittu uyiraiyēṟṟi muṭikka. ēṉaippāliṭaṅkaḷukku itaṉmēlē vikutivēṟupāṭallatu vicēṭamillai yeṉka. malaiyāṉ, malaiyaḷ, malaiyāḷ, malaiyar, malaiyār, malaiyēṉ, malaiyēṉ, malaiyēm, malaiyāy, malaiyīr eṉa ēṉaiyiṭaṅkaḷilum oṭṭikkaṇṭukoḷka. nilattaṉeṉṉum pakupata muṭiyumāṟāvatu:- ivviṭattaṉe/ṉum poruṇmaitōṉṟa, `tattam ... pakutiyākum' eṉpataṉāṉē nilameṉṉum iṭappakutiyai mutalilvaittu, ataṉmītē, `aṉ āṉ aḷ āḷ' eṉpataṉāṉē aṉṉeṉṉum vikutiyai niṟuvi, "ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟkilakkaṇam" eṉpataṉāṉē iṭaiyē takaraiṭainilaiyai varuvittu "mavvīṟoṟṟaḻintu" eṉpataṉāṉē makaravīṟṟaikkeṭuttu, "iyalpiṉum vitiyiṉu niṉṟa vuyirmuṉ, kacatapa mikum" eṉpataṉāṉē takarattai mikuttu, "uṭaṉmēluyir vantu" eṉpataṉāṉē uyiraiyēṟṟi nilattaṉeṉa muṭikka. nilattateṉpataṟkut tuvvikutiyainiṟuvi, akaraccāriyaiyai varuvittut takarattaimikuttu muṉpōla muṭikka. oḻintaṉa muṭipu okkum; vikuti vēṟeṉka: nilattaḷ, nilattar, nilatta, nilattēṉ, nilattēm, nilattāy, nilattīreṉa ellāppāliṭaṅkaḷilum oṭṭuka. paraṇiyāṉeṉṉum pakupata muṭiyumāṟāvatu :- paraṇināḷiṟ piṟantāṉeṉṉum poruṇmaitōṉṟa `muṉpōlap pakutivikuti tantu, `iī aivaḻi yavvum' eṉpataṉāṉ yakaravuṭampaṭumeyyai varuvittu uyirēṟṟi muṭikka. paraṇiyatu eṉpataṟku, muṉpōlat tuvveṉṉum vikutiyum akaraccāriyaiyum uṭampaṭumeyyuṅ koṭuttu uyirēṟṟi muṭikka. paraṇiyāḷ, paraṇiyār eṉa ēṉaippāliṭaṅkaḷilum oṭṭikkoḷka. mārpāṉeṉṉum pakupatam muṭiyumāṟāvatu:- ivvuṟuppaiyuṭaiyāṉeṉṉum poruṇmaitōṉṟa, tattam...pakutiyākum" eṉpataṉāṉē mārpeṉṉum ciṉaippakutiyai mutalilvaittu, "aṉ āṉ" eṉpataṉāl āṉvikutiyainiṟuvi, "uyirvari ṉukkuṟaṇ meyviṭṭōṭum" eṉpataṉāṉē kuṟṟukarattaikkeṭuttu, "uṭaṉmē luyirvan toṉṟuva tiyalpē" eṉpataṉāṉē uyirēṟṟi mārpāṉeṉa muṭikka. mārpatu eṉpataṟkut tuvveṉṉum vikutiyaiyum akaraccāriyaiyum tantu ukaraṅkeṭuttu uyirēṟṟimuṭikka. mārpāḷ, mārpāreṉa ēṉaippāliṭaṅkaḷiṉum oṭṭikkoḷka. kariyaṉeṉṉum pakupatam muṭiyumāṟāvatu:- inniṟattaiyuṭaiyāṉeṉṉum poruṇmaitōṉṟa "cemmai ciṟumai" eṉṉum cūttirattāṟ karumaiyeṉṉum paṇpuppakutiyai mutalilvaittu, "aṉṉāṉ eṉpataṉāṉē aṉṉeṉṉum vikutiyai niṟuvi, "īṟupōta liṭaiyukaramiyyātal" eṉpataṉāṟ pakutiyatu īṟṟuyir meyyaikkeṭuttu ukarattai ikaramākki uṭampaṭumeyyai varuvittu uṭaṉmēluyirēṟṟi muṭikka. kariyatu eṉpataṟku, tuvvikutiyum akaraccāriyaiyum yakaravuṭampaṭumeyyum koṭuttu muṭikka. kariyaḷ, kariyar, kariyaṉa, kariyēṉ, kariyēm, kariyāy, kariyīr eṉa ēṉaippāliṭaṅkaḷilum oṭṭikkoḷka. ūṇaṉ eṉṉum pakupatam muṭiyumāṟāvatu:- ittoḻilaiyuṭaiyāṉeṉṉum poruṇmai tōṉṟa, "tattam ...pakutiyākum" eṉpataṉāṉē, ūṇeṉṉum toḻiṟpakutiyai muṉvaittu, ataṉmītē "aṉṉāṉ" eṉpataṉāṉē aṉṉeṉṉum vikutiyainiṟuvi, "uṭaṉmēl ... iyalpē" eṉpataṉāl, uṭaṉmēluyirēṟṟi muṭikka. ūṇatu eṉpataṟku, tuvvikutiyaiyum akaraccāriyaiyum tantu muṭikka. ūṇaḷ, ūṇar eṉa ēṉaippāliṭaṅkaḷilum oṭṭikkoḷka. naṭantāṉeṉṉum poḻutukoḷ viṉaippakupatam muṭiyumāṟāvatu:- "naṭavā maṭicī viṭukū" eṉpataṉāṉē naṭaveṉṉum pakutiyai muṉvaittu, ataṉmītē, "aṉṉāṉaḷḷāḷ" eṉpataṉāṉē āṉeṉṉum vikutiyai niṟuvi, "taṭaṟavoṟṟiṉṉē" eṉpataṉāṉē iṭaiyē takara iṭainilaiyaivaruvittu, "iyalpiṉum vitiyiṉum" eṉpataṉāṉē takarattai mikuttu, "valittaṉ melitta ṉīṭṭal kuṟukkal" eṉpataṉāṉē takarattai nakaramākki uṭaṉmēluyirēṟṟi naṭantāṉeṉamuṭikka. naṭantatu eṉpataṟkut tuvvikutiyaiyum akaraccāriyaiyuṅ koṭuttut takaravoṟṟai varuvittut takarattaimikuttut takaravoṟṟai nakaramāka melittu muṭikka. naṭantāḷ, naṭantār eṉa ēṉaippāliṭaṅkaḷilum oṭṭikkoḷka. naṭattiṉāṉeṉpataṟku, "viḷampiya pakutivē ṟātalum vitiyē" eṉpataṉāṉē, naṭattu eṉṉum pakutiyaiyākki āṉ vikutiyai niṟuvi iṉ eṉṉum iṭainilaikoṭuttu ukaraṅkeṭuttu uyirēṟṟimuṭikka. naṭattuvittāṉeṉpataṟku, "ceyyeṉ viṉaivaḻivippi taṉivariṉ" eṉpataṉāṉē naṭattuvi eṉṉum pakutiyai ākki, āṉvikutiyai niṟuvit takaraviṭainilaiyai varuvittu, "iyalpiṉum vitiyiṉum" eṉpataṉāṉē takarattai iraṭṭittu uyirēṟṟimuṭikka. vantāṉeṉpataṟku "naṭavāmaṭi" eṉpataṉāṉ, vā eṉ pakutiyai mutalilvaittu ataṉai, "viḷampiya ... vitiyē" eṉpataṉāṉ vam eṉṟākki āṉvikutiyai niṟuvi, "taṭaṟaṭavoṟṟiṉṉē" eṉpataṉāṉē takaraviṭainilaiyaivaruvittu, "mavvī ṟoṟṟaḻintuyirī ṟoppavum" eṉpataṉāṉē makarattai nakaramākki vantāṉeṉa muṭikka. varuvittāṉeṉpataṟku, "viḷampiyapakuti" eṉpataṉāṉ, vāveṉpatai varu eṉṟākki, "ceyyeṉviṉaivaḻi" eṉpataṉāṉē varuvi eṉpatu pakutiyākki, āṉvikutiyum takaraviṭai nilaiyum tantu, "iyalpiṉum vitiyiṉum ... mikum" eṉpataṉāṉē takarattai iraṭṭittu uyirēṟṟi muṭikka. ivviṉaippatamē viṉaippeyarām. avai eṭuttal paṭuttalōcaikaḷāl aṟintukoḷka. piṟavum iṅṅaṉamuṭikka.

iṉi, aracuttoḻilaiyuṭaiyāṉ aracaṉ, vāṇikamuṭaiyāṉ vāṇikaṉ, uḻavaiyuṭaiyāṉ uḻavaṉ, vēḷāṇmai ceyvāṉ vēḷāḷaṇ, kaṇakkālmuyaṉṟuṇpāṉ kaṇakkaṉ, kuntat toḻiluṇpāṉ kuntavaṉ, valaiyālmuyaṉṟuṇpāṉ valaiyaṉ, uvaccattoḻilāl muyaṉṟuṇpāṉ uvaccaṉ, taccattoḻilāl muyaṉṟuṇpāṉ taccaṉ, vaṇṇārattoḻilāl muyaṉṟuṇpāṉ vaṇṇāṉ, kaṇavāḷattoḻilāl muyaṉṟuṇpāṉ kaṇavāḷaṉeṉṟu ivvāṟē āṇpāṟpeyarp pakupataṅkaḷellām muṭikka. peṇpāṟpeyarp pakupatam muṭippuḻaṭa, iṉṉakulattāḷeṉṉum poruṇmaitōṉṟa muṭikka. araciyeṉṉum pakupata muṭiyumāṟāvatu:- "tattam ... pakutiyākum" eṉpataṉāṉē aracu eṉṉum pakutiyait tantu, "aṉṉāṉ" eṉpataṉāṉē ikaravikutiyai niṟuvi, "uyirvariṉuk kuṟaḷ" eṉpataṉāṉē ukarattaik keṭuttuc cakaravoṟṟiṉmēl "uṭaṉmēluyirvantu" eṉpataṉāṉē ikaravuyiraiyēṟṟi muṭikka. pārppaṉiyeṉṉum pakupatam muṭiyumamāṟāvatu:- `tattam pakāppatam' eṉpataṉāṉē pārppāṉeṉṉum pakutiyai mutalvaittu, ataṉai "viḷampiyapakuti" eṉpataṉāṉē pārppaṉeṉa aṉṉīṟākki, aṉṉāṉ" eṉpataṉāṉē ikaravikutiyaik koṇarntu uyirēṟṟi muṭikka. vāṇicciyeṉṉum pakupatam muṭiyumāṟāvatu:- vāṇikaṉ eṉṉum pakutiyai, "viḷampiyapakuti" eṉpataṉāṉē kaṉkeṭuttu niṟuvi ikara vikutiyaik koṇarntu, c eṉṉum iṭainilaiyai varuvittu ataṉai mikuvittu uyirēṟṟimuṭikka. uḻattiyeṉṉum pakupatam muṭiyumāṟāvatu:- uḻavaṉ eṉṉum pakutiyai, "viḷampiya pakuti" eṉpataṉāṉē vaṉkeṭuttuniṟuvi ikaravikuti koṇarntu, "ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇa miyampaliṉ" eṉpataṉāṉē takaraviṭainilaiyai varuvittu ataṉaimikuttu uyirēṟṟi muṭikka. taccicciyeṉṉum pakupatam muṭiyumāṟāvatu:- taccaṉ eṉṉum pakutiyai aṉkeṭuttuniṟuvi ikaravikutiyaik koṇarntu, "ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟku" eṉpataṉāṉ icceṉṉumiṭainilaiyai varuvittu uyirēṟṟi muṭikka. ivvāṟē ellāp pakupataṅkaḷaiyum pakutimutalāṉa karuvikaḷ tantu ēṟṟavāṟu muṭikka.

{{pi-m}} poruḷ uyirēṟṟi varuvittumuṉpōla muṟpōṟ varuvittāṉeṉpataṉai uḻavaimuyalvāṉ veḷḷāṇmai kuntaṉ āṉkeṭuttu aṉniṟuvip pārppaṉeṉa ākki

145

iṭaiyi ṉāṉku mīṟṟi liraṇṭum
allā vaccai varukka mutalīṟu
yavvāti nāṉmai ḷavvāku maiyaim
potuveḻut toḻinta nālēḻun tiriyum.

e-ṉ, vaṭamoḻippatam tamiḻoṭuvaruṅkāl tirivaṉavun tiriyātaṉavum potuvakaiyāṉ uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): vaṭamoḻiyuḷ acceṉṟuvaḻaṅkum uyirpatiṉāṟaṉuḷḷum iṭaiyiṉiṉṟa ēḻāmuyir mutaṉāṉkum īṟṟiṉiṉṟa iraṇṭumāṉa āṟum oḻintu niṉṟa aā iī uū ē ai o au eṉṉum pattum, alleṉṟu vaḻaṅkumey muppattēḻaṉuḷḷum kacaṭatapa veṉṉum aintaṉ varukkattuḷ iṭaiyil urappiyum eṭuttum kaṉaittum collappaṭṭuniṟkum mūṉṟu moḻinta kaṅa, caña, ṭaṇa, tana, pama eṉṉum pattum, yaralava eṉṉum nāṉkum ḷavvumāṉa irupattaintum tamiḻmoḻikkum vaṭamoḻikkum potuvām; ivaiyaṉṟi, mēluyiriloḻinta āṟum, aintuvarukkaṅkaḷilum iṭaikaḷiloḻinta patiṉaintum, muppatāmey mutalāṉa eṭṭaṉuḷ ḷakaramoḻinta ēḻumāṉa irupatteṭṭum vaṭamoḻikkē uriyavāyt tamiḻmoḻikkuvaruṅkāl tamakkēṟṟa potuveḻuttukkaḷākat tirintu varum e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): irupattaintum potuveṉavē maṟṟuḷḷavai ciṟappeḻuttāmeṉpatu tāṉē pōtarumeṉak koḷka. ivvāṟu pakutturaittaṟkup payaṉ :- potuveḻuttuppatam varutalē ciṟappuṭaittu, allaṉavarutal ciṟappaṉṟeṉak koḷka. vaṭamoḻi muṭipilakkaṇaṅkūṟātu tiripu kūṟiyatu eṉṉaiyōveṉiṉ, tamiḻuḷ viravivarumāttiraikkut tiripu kūṟiyataṉṟi avaitam ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉ aḵtoḻinta ticaiccoṟkaḷukkum ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟal vēṇṭum; vēṇṭavē, tamiḻkūṟuvāṉ pukuntuvaittu īṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkūṟiṉ maṟṟoṉṟu virittaleṉṉum nūṟkuṟṟamēyām. ātalāl, avai vaḻaṅkumāṟaṟitaṟku vēṇṭum tiripē coṉṉā reṉka.

{{pi-m}} āriyamoḻippatam tamiḻil ciṟappuṭaiya vaṭavilakkaṇaṅkūṟātu ataṉ ātikkaṇṇētamiḻ kūṟuvāṉ

avaṟṟuḷ,
ēḻāmuyi riyyu miruvumai varukkat
tiṭaiyiṉ mūṉṟu mavvam mutalum
eṭṭē yavvu muppatu cayavum
5 mēloṉṟu caṭavu miraṇṭu catavum
mūṉṟē yakavu maintiru kavvum
āvī ṟaiyu mīyī ṟikaramum.

e-ṉ, mēṟpotuvakaiyāṟcoṉṉa tiripuvikārattaip pakuttuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

((WFW)): mēṟcoṉṉa ciṟappeḻuttukkaḷuḷ ēḻāmuyir ikaramum iruvumām; aintuvarukkaṅkaḷuḷḷum iṭaiyilniṉṟa mūṉṟum avvav varukkaṅkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttām; eṭṭāmeyyāṉa cavvarukkattuḷ eṭuttuccollumey orōviṭattu yakaramumām; muppatāmey moḻi mutaṟkaṭ cakaramum iṭaikkaṇ yakaramumām; muppattoṉṟāmey moḻi mutaṟkaṭ cavvum iṭaikkaṇ ṭavvumām; muppattiraṇṭāmey moḻi mutaṟkaṭ cakaramum iṭaikkaṇ takaramumamām; muppattumūṉṟāmey moḻi mutaṟkaṇ akaramum iṭaikkaṭ kakaramumām; muppattaintāmeyyāṉa kūṭṭeḻuttu iṟutikeṭṭu iraṇṭu kakaramumām; ākāravīṟṟumoḻiyuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ īṟṟuākāram aikāramām; īkāravīṟṟumoḻiyuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ īṟṟu īkāram ikaramām e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): mēleṉṟatu, muppatiṉmēlāṉa oṉṟu iraṇṭu mūṉṟu aintu kōṭaṟkeṉka.

va-ṟu. iṭapam, viṭapam, tiṭṭi eṉavum; iruṭi, mirukam, virutti eṉavum ēḻāmuyir ikaramum iruvumāyiṟṟu. nakam, nākam, mēkam; calavāti, vicayam, caruccarai; pīṭam, pīṭai, tiṭam; talam, tiṉam, tarai; palam, pantam, pārameṉa aintuvarukkattilum urappiyum eṭuttum kaṉaittum collumūṉṟum avvam mutalāyiṉa. ayam, paṅkayameṉa eṭṭāmey yakaramumāyiṟṟu. cālai, cūlai eṉavum, mayāṉameṉavum muppatāmey cakaramum yakaramumāyiṟṟu. caṭṭi, iṭapam eṉa muppattoṉṟāmey cakaramum ṭakaramumāyiṟṟu. citti, cūttiram, cēṉai eṉavum, cātaṉam, vattarāyaṉ (vatsarājaṉ - utayaṇaṉ) eṉavum muppattiraṇṭāmey cakaramum takaramumāyiṟṟu. araṉ, ari, āram eṉavum, arukaṉ, ciṅkam, mōkam eṉavum muppattumūṉṟāmey akaramum kakaramu māyiṟṟu. pakkam, takkaṇam (teṟku - pākatac citaivu) eṉa muppattaintām kūṭṭeḻuttu īṟu keṭṭu iraṇṭu kakaramāyiṟṟu. vēlai, cālai, mālai, uvamai, vaṉitai eṉa ākāravīṟu aikāramāyiṟṟu. puri, mētiṉi, kumāri, nītiṉi eṉa īkāravīṟu ikaramāyiṟṟu. ivai potuveḻuttātal tōṟṟaṟkuc cūttirattāṟ pirittōtiṉāreṉka.

{{pi-m}} iypumām ayaṉ, paṅkayaṉ cāṭṭuvalam, viṭam

ravviṟ kammuta lāmuk kuṟilum
lavviṟ kimmuta liraṇṭum yavviṟ
kiyyu moḻimuta lākimuṉ varumē. ituvumatu.

((WFW)): rakāra lakāra yakāraṅkaḷai mutalākavuṭaiya moḻikaḷiṉ mēl immūṉṟukuṟṟuyirum coṉṉavāṟuvarum e-ṟu.

((VIRI)): va-ṟu. araṅkam, irāmaṉ, irāvaṇaṉ, urōmam eṉavum; ilaṅkai, ilāpam, ulōpam eṉavum; iyakkaṉ, iyāttirai eṉavum muṟaiyē kāṇka.
